✨Features

🎁Sell Chest - Premium

The sell chest feature is not currently introduced as a core function of UltimateShop and is still in the early testing phase. It may be officially released in future versions or potentially removed if it contains irreparable critical issues.
Sell chest is now available at 4.2.0b or later version.

Sell chest placed before you update to 4.2.6 version will no longer work after upgrade, This is due to a change in the logic for storing the location of the sell chest. You can ask the player to replace the sell chest to resolve this issue.

Limitation

Folia server does NOT support this feature.

Sell chest cannot form a double chest with other chests.

The data and location of sell chest is stored in the world itself, and the plugin itself does not store sell chest. If your world is damaged, you cannot recover the relevant data of sell chest.

If your server crashes unexpectedly and the world experiences a rollback, then the same applies to sell chest. However, your economy plugin may store the profits generated by players selling items, so there is a risk of duplicate economy in this situation.

Sell chest only supports listening common changes made to the blocks in vanilla, such as block break, explosions, etc. If there are unexpected situations or changes made to the blocks by other plugins, it may result in the sell chest actually being destroyed in the world, but the plugin didn't find this and still think exists as a chest. If you enable the hologram, you may find that the hologram still exists, but the chest has been damaged.

It is not possible to calculate the total earn of sell chest because UltimateShop supports multiple price types for a single product, rather than many plugins of the same type having one item stack corresponding to a double type Vault price. If that's the case, then only the logic of adding numbers can be implemented, but unfortunately, UltimateShop not only supports products Vault prices, you can even use custom fake prices, so it is very hard to calculate total earn, but last earn is supported to display in hologram.

You may also discover other areas that have not been implemented as expected.

Note

Sell chest will only work both the chunk is loaded and the owner is online. No plan to support multi players share same sell chest.

Only players who place sell chests will be considered owners. But all players can destroy the sell chest.

Products that using dynamic price is also supported in sell chest. For example, you set each time the sell price decreases by $1, with a base price of $10, selling X units of this product would result in a total of $10 + $9 + $8 + $7 + $6 + $5, etc.

Config

All sell chest configs are stored at sell_chests foler, an example of it's config is like below:

The display item of sell chest ONLY support use CHEST as material. Otherwise, it won't work!

The most thing is same as Sell Stick, I will only tell you about different things:

holograms: The content of the hologram shown above the sell chest. Requires an enabled supported hologram plugin.

y-offset: The height deviation of the hologram compared to the sell chest can be adjusted according to the number of rows in the hologram.

Hologram placeholders

Placeholder

Display Info

{id}

Sell chest config ID.

{owner}

Username of the player who placed the sell chest.

{usage}

Remaining usages, or the configured infinite text for an infinite sell chest.

{multiplier}

Sell price multiplier.

{price}

Earnings from the last successful selling attempt, or the configured price-empty text if there is no history.

Setting in config.yml file

enabled: Whether use this feature. If you do not need this feature and want to has better performance, you can try disable this option.

debug: Will pring debug info at console related to sell chest after enable.

perior-ticks and batch-count:

period-ticks: 600

Controls how often the sell chest system runs. In this example:
The value is in Minecraft ticks (20 ticks = 1 second).

600 ticks = 30 seconds

Every 30 seconds, the system processes one batch of sell chests.

batch-count: 5

Controls how many batches all sell chests are divided into per cycle. In this example:

All sell chests are split into 5 batches

Only one batch is processed each period

A full cycle requires 5 executions

The purpose of doing this is to save server performance. We will not set separate loop timers for each sell chest to avoid server performance degradation caused by too many sell chests, nor will we arrange all sell chests in the same loop timer to cause server lag.

send-sell-message: Whether we will send message after each sell.

price-empty: The text displayed in hologram if there is no history for the sell chest.

hologram: Settings for the hologram feature.

enabled: Whether display holgoram above sell chest.

plugin: Set hologram plugin. For list of supported plugin, please click here.

Showcase

Previous🪄Sell Stick - Premium

Next💾Saved Item (Item Manager)

Last updated 2 months ago

Limitation

Note

Config

Hologram placeholders

Setting in config.yml file

Showcase

Copy

display-item:
material: CHEST
name: '&dSell Chest &7(500 times)'
lore:
- '&fPlace this chest in your world to sell items!'
- ''
- '&cLeft usages: {times}'

usage-times: 500

multiplier: 1.2

y-offset: 2.25
holograms:
- '&e&lSell chest'
- '&7ID: {id}'
- '&7Owner: {owner}'
- '&7Left Times: {usage}'
- '&7Multiplier: {multiplier}'
- '&7Last Earn: {price}'

actions:
1:
type: sound
sound: 'block.note_block.pling'

conditions: []

Copy

sell-chest:
enabled: true
debug: false
period-ticks: 600
batch-count: 5
send-sell-message: true
price-empty: '{lang}'
hologram:
enabled: true
# Support value: DecentHolograms, CMI
plugin: 'DecentHolograms'

✨Features

🧪Example: Sell Income Limits

This example uses vault-sell.yml from Easy Prices so all products referencing it share the same per-player Vault income caps (default: 2,000 per 5 hours, 10,000 daily, 50,000 weekly).

Custom placeholders are available in the free version (up to 5), so this template works in both the free and premium versions.

Usage

Reference vault-sell in sell-prices on every product that should share the same Vault sell limits:

Copy

sell-prices:
1:
shared-price: vault-sell
amount: 25

Default Caps and Reset Windows

Period

Default cap

Counter

Reset

Every 5 hours

2,000

{custom_sell_5h}

5 hours after the first recorded payout in the window

Daily

10,000

{custom_sell_daily}

Midnight (00:00) every day

Weekly

50,000

{custom_sell_weekly}

Midnight (00:00) every Monday

How It Works and Lore Display

Condition timing: type: placeholder in require-conditions evaluates expressions after sell multipliers and rounding. When multiple price entries in the same sale reference the template, they are checked against their combined payout.

Income tracking: give-actions uses type: add_custom_placeholder to add {amount} to each numeric custom placeholder, with multi-once: true so the full reward is recorded once per price entry.

Resets & countdowns: Numeric custom placeholders support TIMER, TIMED, and CUSTOM reset modes. Use {custom-times_sell_daily} to show the next reset time.

Lore display: The default language file uses @r[vault-sell] lines in display-item.add-lore to show used quota for all three periods below the sell price. See Display Item Add Lore.

How Sell All works out how many times to sell

The default vault-sell.yml includes a max-sell-amount formula that checks the remaining room under all three caps and divides the tightest remaining allowance by the actual payout per trade. For example, if the tightest cap has 80 coins left and each trade pays 5 coins, Sell All allows at most 16 trades (or 10 if the player only has stock for 10).

To change a cap, just edit that condition's value—the formula reads it automatically. Only update the formula if you change which counter or comparison rule is used.

The default formula uses min(), which requires math.enable-function in config.yml. To write your own formula, see max-sell-amount in the Single Entry reference.

Adjust or remove the limits

All three caps are enforced by require-conditions in vault-sell.yml. There is no separate toggle; edit that section directly:

Common adjustments:

Remove all limits: delete the entire require-conditions section. The template keeps counting and displaying used quota without blocking sales.

Keep only some periods: delete the entries you do not want (for example, keep only the 5-hour cap).

Raise the caps: increase value (for example, '999999999').

Bypass for one product: add require-conditions: false on that product's price entry to clear the inherited conditions.

Even after removing require-conditions, give-actions still increments sell_5h, sell_daily, and sell_weekly, so the displayed quota continues to grow without blocking sales. To hide the display as well, remove the @r[vault-sell] lines from display-item.add-lore in your language file.

How Sell All works out how many times to sell

Copy

require-conditions:
1:
type: placeholder
placeholder: '{custom_sell_5h} + {amount}'
rule: '<='
value: '2000'

✨Features

🪄Sell Stick - Premium

It is not possible to calculate the total earn of sell stick because UltimateShop supports multiple price types for a single product, rather than many plugins of the same type having one item stack corresponding to a double type Vault price. If that's the case, then only the logic of adding numbers can be implemented, but unfortunately, UltimateShop not only supports products Vault prices, you can even use custom fake prices, so it is very hard to calculate total earn.

Products that using dynamic price is also supported in sell stick. For example, you set each time the sell price decreases by $1, with a base price of $10, selling X units of this product would result in a total of $10 + $9 + $8 + $7 + $6 + $5, etc.

All sell stick configs are stored in sell_sticks folder. The file name is it's ID, for example: A.yml means it's ID is A. An example of it's config is like below:

Copy

display-item:
material: STICK
name: '&dSell Stick &7(5 times)'
lore:
- '&fRight click a chest to use this item!'
- ''
- '&cLeft usages: {times}'

usage-times: 5

multiplier: 1.2

actions:
1:
type: sound
sound: 'block.note_block.pling'

conditions: []

display-item: The display item of sell stick. Should use Item Format.

useage-times: Maxium usage times of this item. If this option value is less than 0 or does not exist, we will make this sell stick is infinite.

multiplier: The multiplier of sell stick.

actions: The action will execute after use this sell stick. Should use Action format.

conditions: The condition player need meet to use this sell stick. Should use Condition Format.

After confiure the sell stick, you can reload the plugin then use /shop givesellstick to obtain sell stick item. For more info, please view here.

UltimateShop supports you prevent player use sell tick in protected area or other player's land.(OP players will not check this) You can check Supported Protection Plugins list here. You can use permission ultimateshop.bypass.protection to bypass protection check for sell stick use.

📋Format

🎬Action Format

The action format will consist of several options.

The actions in the Action Format example only represent Action Format start from here. Please refer to the page description of the corresponding function for specific option names, such as buy-actions.

General Options

Apply Times

This action will run only when player have buy/sell spcified times product.

Optional, if not set, will execute action every time.

start-apply: Start which times this action will apply. Optional. Default to 0.

end-apply: Last times the action will apply. Optional. Default to infinite.

apply: Which times this action will apply, format: [1,2,3,4]. Optional. Default use start-apply option value.

Copy

actions:
1:
apply: [1,2,3,4,5]
start-apply: 1
end-apply: 5

Sell All Once / Multi Once

When multiple quantity / products are about to be sold, adding this option means that only the first product's action will be executed. Very useful for sounds action, if you didn't add this, all product's sound action will execute.

Open Once

Only work for menu's open-actions option, if enabled, only the menu opened by the player for the first time will trigger this action, which means that if the opened menu was opened through another menu, this action cannot be triggered.

Click Type

This action only executed when player use this click type to active this action. Won't work for open-actions, close-action in menu configs and buy-actions, sell-actions in product configs.

Optional, if not set, this action will execute for each click type.

Java Only/Bedrock Only - PREMIUM

This action only executed if player is java or bedrock players.

Optional, if not set, both java and bedrock players will execute this action.

Fail Type

Only support fail-actions in product configs.

Supported fail type:

ERROR

PERMISSION

PLAYER_MAX

SERVER_MAX

REQUIRE_CONDITION_NOT_MEET

NOT_ENOUGH

INVENTORY_FULL

API_CANCEL

Available Placeholders

{world}

{amount}

Item-Level buy-actions and sell-actions

When you use buy-actions or sell-actions on the item itself, {amount} means the item-level transaction amount prepared by the buy or sell flow.

In practice:

if display-item.calculate-amount is false, {amount} is always 1 for the item action layer

if display-item.calculate-amount is true, {amount} is transaction quantity * display item amount

Example:

display item amount: 1

player buys 5

item buy-actions sees {amount} = 5

Another example:

display item amount: 64

player buys 5

item buy-actions sees {amount} = 320

Single Thing give-actions and take-actions

When you use give-actions or take-actions on a single thing, {amount} also means the real final amount of that specific single thing.

{player_x}

{player_y}

{player_z}

{player_pitch}

{player_yaw}

{player}

{item} - Product ID

{item-name} - Product Display Name

{shop} - Shop ID

{shop-name} - Shop Display Name

{shop-menu} - Shop's Menu ID

Sound

Send sound to player.

Message

Send a message to the player, support color code.

Title

Send title to the player, support the color code.

Particle

Announcement

Send a message to all online players, support color code.

Effect

Give players potion effect.

Teleport

Teleport player to specified location.

Player Command

Make the player excutes a command.

Op Command

Make the player excutes a command as OP.

Console Command

Make the console excutes a command.

Spawn vanilla mobs

Spawn vanilla mobs.

MythicMobs spawn

Require MythicMobs.

Open Common Menu

Open specified common menus.

Open Shop Menu

Open Buy More Menu

Open Buy More Menu with Custom Buy More Menu settings - Premium

Open Sell All Menu

Buy Product

amount: '{amount}' resolves the action context's quantity. In custom product click events it uses default-buy-amount, including sub-button overrides. Explicit amounts remain fixed; omitting amount defaults to 1. Resolved amounts must be integers from 1 to 9999.

Sell Product

amount: '{amount}' resolves the action context's quantity. In custom product click events it uses default-sell-amount, including sub-button overrides. This can differ from the default buy amount. Explicit amounts remain fixed; omitting amount defaults to 1. sell-all: true retains the existing maximum-sell behavior.

Close

Close the inventory.

Delay - Premium

Make the action run after X ticks.

Chance - Premium

Set the chance the action will be excuted, up to 100. 50 means this action has 50% chance to excute.

Any - Premium

Randomly choose specified amount of actions to execute.

Conditional - Premium

Only players meet the conditions you set here will be able to execute the action.

Connect - Premium

Require enable bungeecord-sync.enabled option in config.yml and correctly set the BungeeCord settings. For more info, please view Multi Server Sync page.

Update GUI

Update all buttons in opened GUI. Will not update gui title.

Update GUI Title - Premium

Require server enable title update feature, for more info, please view this page.

Add Favourite - Premium

Add the product to favourite list.

Remove Favourite - Premium

Remove this product from favourite list.

Toggle Favourite - Premium

Toggle the product favourite status, if it is exist, we will remove, if not exist, we will add.

Prompt - Premium

Asks the player to type something in chat before continuing with follow-up actions.

{arg}

The full raw input string.

If the player enters:

then:

{arg_1}, {arg_2}, {arg_3} ...

Whitespace-split arguments.

If the player enters:

then:

Back

Back to the previous menu, if previous menu does not exist, will open the menu exist in menu option.

📋Format

⚖️Condition Format

The condition format will consist of several options.

The conditions in the Condition Format example only represent Condition Format start from here. Please refer to the page description of the corresponding function for specific option names, such as buy-conditions.

General Options

Apply Times

This condition will only check when player have buy/sell spcified times product.

start-apply: Start which times this condition will apply. Optional. Default to 0.

end-apply: Last times the condition will apply. Optional. Default to infinite.

apply: Which times this condition will apply, format: [1,2,3,4]. Optional. Default use start-apply option value.

Copy

conditions:
1:
apply: [1,2,3,4,5]
start-apply: 1
end-apply: 5

Click Type

This condition only checked when player use this click type to use the button.

Copy

conditions:
1:
click-type: LEFT

Actions - Premium

You should use fail-actions in products config if you want to modify the product buy or sell condition not meet message.

You can set actions for the entire condition, and if you want to do that, it's like this:

Available Placeholders

{world}

{amount}

Item-Level buy-conditions and sell-conditions

When you use buy-conditions or sell-conditions on the item itself, {amount} means the number of times the player is trying to buy or sell that item.

If the player buys 5, then {amount} is 5.

Single Thing apply-conditions and Legacy conditions

When you use apply-conditions on a single thing, {amount} is always 1.

The same is true for legacy conditions, because in code they work as the old form of apply-conditions.

Why?

apply-conditions are checked before the plugin finishes selecting the final single things

at that stage, the real calculated amount is not ready yet

So in this layer:

{amount} does not mean the final price

{amount} does not mean the final product amount

{amount} is just 1

This means apply-conditions are for choosing branches, not for checking the real calculated amount.

Single Thing require-conditions

When you use require-conditions on a single thing, {amount} means the final calculated amount of that selected single thing

This is the most important difference from apply-conditions.

{player_x}

{player_y}

{player_z}

{player_pitch}

{player_yaw}

{player}

{item} - Product ID

{item-name} - Product Display Name

{shop} - Shop ID

{shop-name} - Shop Display Name

{shop-menu} - Shop's Menu ID

World

Player must be in the world.

Biome

Player must be in the biome.

Permission

Player must has the permission.

Remember that OP players will always have all permissions unless plugin set it not by default, so if you want to test this condition, you have to deop yourself.

Placeholder

Player must be meet the placeholder condition.

Rule can be set to:

>=

<=

>

<

== (String)

= (Number)

!= (Number or string)

!*= (Number or string) Not contains.

*= (String) Contains, for example, str *= string is true, but example *= ple is false.

Menu

Player is opening the specified menu.

Menu Type

Player is opening the specified type of menu.

Supported menu type:

COMMON

SHOP

MORE

SEARCH

FAVOURITE

Any - Premium

Not - Premium

📋Format

🖼️Display Item Format

You can use display item format in menu button and shop product's display-item option.

Single

If your display item is only one and does not have any condition to display it, simply use Item Format in this section!

Copy

B:
display-item:
material: BREAD
name: '&cSuper Bread'

Multi

If you want set multi display item for this button or product and use condition system to make us choose one of it to display, simply use this new format as below:

Copy

1:
display-item:
default:
material: GREEN_WOOL
amount: 1
name: '&eDay 1'
lore:
- '&fToday Reward:'
- '&7 - 10 Gems'
- ''
- '&#FFFACDClick to claim!'
claimed:
material: RED_WOOL
amount: 1
name: '&eDay 1'
lore:
- '&fIncluded Reward:'
- '&7 - 10 Gems'
- ''
- '&#ff3300Already claimed!'
display-item-conditions:
claimed: # Condition ID
1: # Means first condition
type: placeholder
placeholder: '%ultimateshop_streak_A_{buy-times-player}%'
rule: '>'
value: '0'
2: # You can add more condition with Condition Format
type: world
world: 'testCondition'
default:
1:
type: placeholder
placeholder: '%ultimateshop_streak_A_{buy-times-player}%'
rule: '='
value: '0'z

📋Format

💹EconomyFormat™

EconomyFormat is a configuration format provided by plugins that can associate with your server economy. If an option in a configuration file requires or supports EconomyFormat, we will annotate it.

Please remember: UltimateShop is just a shop plugin and does not provide custom economy functionality. If you need a custom economy as your server economy, please find a suitable economy plugin yourself. Vault is not an economy plugin, it is just a dependency plugin for many economy plugins. After installing Vault on the server, it is also necessary to install the economy plugins that support it.

Hook Economy

Hook economy use format as below:

economy-plugin: What plugin you want this price economy hook into, for now, UltimateShop supports Vault, GamePoints, PlayerPoints, CoinsEngine, UltraEconomy, EcoBits, PEconomy, RedisEconomy, RoyaleEconomy, VotingPlugin. Required.

economy-type: If economy plugin is multi-currency economy plugin, you have to type currency name here. Required if you set a multi-currency plugin, otherwise it's optional.

amount: Price Amount. Required.

max-amount: Max amount, useful for dynamic prices. Optional.

min-amount: Min amount, useful for dynamic prices.Optional.

Example:

Copy

1:
economy-plugin: Vault
# If you set Economy plugin to CoinsEngine, then:
# economy-plugin: CoinsEngine
# economy-type: Coin
# Yeah, you need add economy-type option here because its a multi-currency plugin.
# For this, players will always cost 5$.
amount: 5
placeholder: '$5'

Vanilla Economy

Vanilla economy use format as below.

economy-type: Supports exp, levels. Required.

amount: Price Amount. Required.

max-amount: Max amount, useful for dynamic prices. Optional.

min-amount: Min amount, useful for dynamic prices.Optional.

Example:

Previous🌉ItemBridge

Next🖼️Display Item Format

Last updated 4 months ago

Hook Economy

Vanilla Economy

Copy

1:
economy-type: levels
amount: 5
placeholder: '5 levels'

📋Format

🛒Info of ItemFormat™

ItemFormat is a configuration format provided by plugins that can generate items in Minecraft games through multi line YAML formatted text. If an option in a configuration file requires or supports ItemFormat, we will annotate it. A classic example is that you can fill in the ItemFormat in buy-prices options, so that the described item can be used as the buy price for the product.

Item parameter possible value list

In ItemFormat, there are some item parameter values that are not arbitrarily filled in. You can only fill in the value of the item parameter that exists in the game. For example, in enchantments, you can only fill in the ID of the enchantment that exists in the game (including newly added enchantments in some custom enchantment plugins and data packs). The following provides possible values for various item parameters.

Each item parameters we will give you a link with <version> arg. Which you need is replace the <version> to your server's Minecraft version, like 1.21.3, 1.20.4, 1.20.6. Please note that PaperAPI does not save each Minecraft version's Paper API, so you after open the link you are going to a strange site which not give you the information you need, don't worry, change the Minecraft version to another and try again.

Flag

Copy

https://jd.papermc.io/paper/<version>/org/bukkit/inventory/ItemFlag.html

Sound

Copy

https://jd.papermc.io/paper/<version>/org/bukkit/JukeboxSong.html

Item Ratity

Copy

https://jd.papermc.io/paper/<version>/org/bukkit/inventory/ItemRarity.html

Enchantments

After 1.20.5 version, we follow use Minecraft's vanilla enchantment ID instead of legacy one used in Spigot API, please go to Minecraft wiki site if you are using 1.20.5+ version.

Copy

https://jd.papermc.io/paper/<version>/org/bukkit/enchantments/Enchantment.html

Attribute ID

Slot

Banner Pattern

Dye Color (includes Banner Pattern Color)

Trim Material

Trim Pattern

Damage Type Tag

Entity Type

Material / Entity Type Tag

Potion Base Effect

After 1.20.5

WATER, MUNDANE, THICK, AWKWARD, NIGHT_VISION, LONG_NIGHT_VISION, INVISIBILITY, LONG_INVISIBILITY, LEAPING, LONG_LEAPING, STRONG_LEAPING, FIRE_RESISTANCE, LONG_FIRE_RESISTANCE, SWIFTNESS, LONG_SWIFTNESS, STRONG_SWIFTNESS, SLOWNESS, LONG_SLOWNESS, STRONG_SLOWNESS, WATER_BREATHING, LONG_WATER_BREATHING, HEALING, STRONG_HEALING, HARMING, STRONG_HARMING, POISON, LONG_POISON, STRONG_POISON, REGENERATION, LONG_REGENERATION, STRONG_REGENERATION, STRENGTH, LONG_STRENGTH, STRONG_STRENGTH, WEAKNESS, LONG_WEAKNESS, LUCK, TURTLE_MASTER, LONG_TURTLE_MASTER, STRONG_TURTLE_MASTER, SLOW_FALLING, LONG_SLOW_FALLING, WIND_CHARGED, WEAVING, OOZING, INFESTED.

Before 1.20.5

AWKWARD, FIRE_RESISTANCE, INSTANT_DAMAGE, INSTANT_HEAL, INVISIBILITY, JUMP, LUCK, MUNDANE, NIGHT_VISION, POISON, REGEN, SLOW_FALLING, SLOWNESS, SPEED, STRENGTH, THICK, TURTLE_MASTER, UNCRAFTABLE, WATER, WATER_BREATHING, WEAKNESS.

📋Format

🌉ItemBridge

This plugin supports use ItemBridge by jhqwqmc as custom item provider which supports more custom item plugins than us.

Supported Plugin List

AzureFlow

Baikiruto

CraftEngine

CustomFishing

DragonArmourers

EcoArmor

EcoCrates

EcoItems

EcoMobs

EcoPets

EcoScrolls

ExecutableItems

HeadDatabase

HMCCosmetics

ItemsAdder

MagicGem

MMOItems

MythicMobs

NeigeItems

Nexo

Nova

Oraxen

PxRpg

Ratziel

Reforges

Sertraline

Slimefun

StatTrackers

SX-Item

Talismans

Zaphkiel

Use ItemBridge as custom item provider

Find hook-item-method option in config.yml file and set it to ITEMBRIDGE. Then, restart your server.

And then, use hook-plugin and hook-item option in ItemFormat to obtain the item you want from other plugins.

Please note that built-in default custom item provider in ItemFormat has different hook-item format than ItemBridge. For example:

In built-in custom item provider, CraftEngine hook use this hook-item format (namespace;;id):

In ItemBridge custom item provider, CraftEngine hook use this hook-item format (namespace:id):

If you are unsure how to fill in the values for these two options, you can hold the corresponding item then use command /shop generateitemformat to parse the ItemFormat of it. After execute this command, a new file called generated-item-format.yml wll be generated at plugins/UltimateShop folder. (or plugins/<pluginNameYouAreUsing>)

PreviousTesting Paper Components

Next💹EconomyFormat™

Last updated 4 months ago

Supported Plugin List

Use ItemBridge as custom item provider

Copy

# Support value: DEFAULT or ITEMBRIDGE
hook-item-method: 'DEFAULT'

Copy

hook-plugin: 'CraftEngine'
hook-item: 'myemoji;;happy'

Copy

hook-plugin: 'CraftEngine'
hook-item: 'myemoji:happy'

📋Format

📝ItemFormat™

Mojang very like change material (item type), enchantment, potion and many other things's ID (or namespace key). For example, they changed sweeping enchantment ID to sweeping_edge in 1.21. So, before you update your server version, you need carefully check that whether new versions have changed something, otherwise it will break your shop or menu configs.

Hold a item then use command /shop generateitemformat to parse the ItemFormat of it. After execute this command, a new file called generated-item-format.yml wll be generated at plugins/UltimateShop folder. (or plugins/<pluginNameYouAreUsing>)

Hook Item

This section tells you about the built-in custom item provider, you can use ItemBridge as custom item provider instead which supports more custom item plugins, click here to know more.

Hook Plugin

Possible Value: EcoItems, EcoArmor, MMOItems, ItemsAdder, Oraxen, MythicMobs, eco, NeigeItems, ExecutableItems, Nexo, CraftEngine.

Copy

hook-plugin: MMOItems

Hook Item

For EcoItems, Oraxen, MythicMobs, Nexo, you should write item id.

For ItemsAdder, eco, you should write namespace:item id.

eco's namespace id plugin name, like talismans. The item id follow below rules:

Talisman: Just type talisman ID here.

EcoArmor:

Armor: set_<setID>_<slotID>

Advanced Armor: set_<setID>_<slotID>_advanced

Upgrade Crystal: shard_<shardID>

Reforges: stone_<reforgeID>

For EcoArmor, you should write armor set id;;armor slot. armor slot can be set to BOOTS, CHESTPLATE, ELYTRA, HELMET, LEGGINGS.

For MMOItems, you should write item typeId;;item id.

For CraftEngine, you should write namespace;;item id.

Stop! If you are just trying to get items from custom item plugins, hook-plugin and hook-item option should be enough for you, if your custom item plugins are not supported, please use command instead, all things below is suit for vanilla items, if you set them here, we will replace the related to item attributes and this will lead to the item you get here is different from the item should be in custom item plugins.

Material

If you want to use saved item by plugin, just set this option value to saved item ID, like material: superior_sword.

If the value is empty or illegal, defaults to stone.

Do not use this option when you have hook-plugin option, it won't work if you insist on it!

Amount

Support use PlaceholderAPI or math calculate. For example, %player_health% * 5.

Min Amount/Max Amount - Only support in price/products

When you use dynamic value in amount option, you can use min-amount and max-amount option to limit it's min value and max value. Useful for dynamic price.

Component (1.21.5+, Requires Paper) - Premium

The component section uses Paper's DataComponent API to set item properties. It requires Paper 1.21.5+ and UltimateShop Premium. Newer components require newer server versions; see Component Format for the version table and supported keys.

Keep material and amount at the ItemFormat root. Component values are applied after the root ItemFormat options:

debuild-item-method in config.yml accepts LEGACY or COMPONENT. It controls how /shop generateitemformat and /shop saveitem <ID> itemformat save an item. In COMPONENT mode, supported properties are saved inside component on Paper 1.21.5+. The setting is not required to build an item that already contains this section.

For complete version-specific examples and save/rebuild checks, see Testing Paper Components.

Custom Name/Display Name

According to your configuration file, there are two formats, one is the old version color code used before version 1.9, or the Text Component used in later versions. The former uses a color code format we created, while the latter uses Mini Message format, as detailed here. Mini Message format require your server core is Paper.

Item Name (1.20.5+)

Item Name is a new item attribute added in 1.20.5, different from display name, item name can not be changed in anvil.

According to your configuration file, there are two formats, one is the old version color code used before version 1.9, or the Text Component used in later versions. The former uses a color code format we created, while the latter uses Mini Message format, as detailed here. Mini Message format require your server core is Paper.

Lore

You can use \n to represent line breaks.

According to your configuration file, there are two formats, one is the old version color code used before version 1.9, or the Text Component used in later versions. The former uses a color code format we created, while the latter uses Mini Message format, as detailed here. Mini Message format require your server core is Paper.

Max Stack (1.20.5+)

Food (1.20.5+)

Effects format: Potion Type ID, Duration, Amplifier, Ambient, Particles, Icon, Chance.

For Ambient, Particles, Icon is boolean type arg, and for Chance is a number from 0 to 1.

For convert is Item Format that the item will return to player after eaten this food (Require 1.21+, and this is optional).

Tool (1.21+)

Jukebox Playable (1.21+)

Fire Resistant (1.20.5+)

Hide Tool Tip (1.20.5+)

Unbreakable

Ratity (1.20.5+)

Flags

Enchants

Config section format is: Enchant ID: Enchant Level.

For enchantment book: You maybe need use stored-enchants instead of enchants.

For custom enchantments: Some enchantments plugins are not registered their enchantment into game, so this won't work for them.

You should use Minecraft enchantment ID instead of Spigot's after 1.20.5.

Glow (1.20.5+)

Custom Model Data

Attributes

Please note that 1.20.5 has changed attribute format, if you generated item before 1.20.5, you have to reconfig them into new format.

Each list entry is one attribute modifier. type is the Attribute ID. Entries are applied in list order, and the same attribute type can appear more than once.

Damageable

Represents an item that has durability and can take damage.

Damage

Max Damage (1.20.5+)

Similar to max durability.

Enchantment Storage

This is specific to items that can store enchantments, as opposed to being enchanted. Material.ENCHANTED_BOOK is an example of an item with enchantment storage.

Stored Enchants

Similar to Enchants.

Banner

Patterns

Patterns are applied in list order. The same pattern type can appear more than once.

Potion

Represents a potion or item that can have custom effects.

Base Effect (1.20.5+)

Base Effect (Before 1.20.5)

Format: Potion Type ID, Extended, Upgraded.

For Extended and Upgraded arg is boolean type.

Effects / Custom Effects

Effects format: Potion Type ID, Duration, Amplifier, Ambient, Particles, Icon.

For Ambient, Particles, Icon is boolean type arg.

Color

Color has 2 formats:

Red, Green, Blue

One number (like 0xff0000)

Armor

Represents armor that an entity can equip.

Trim (1.20+)

Config section format is 2 sub options.

Leather Armor

Color

Color has 2 formats:

Red, Green, Blue

One number (like 0xff0000)

Axolotl Bucket (1.17+)

Color

Possible value: LUCY, WILD, GOLD, CYAN, BLUE.

Tropical Fish Bucket (1.14+)

Color

Possible value: WHITE, ORANGE, MAGENTA, LIGHT_BLUE, YELLOW, LIME, PINK, GRAY, LIGHT_GRAY, CYAN, PURPLE, BLUE, BROWN, GREEN, RED, BLACK.

Pattern Color

Similar to Color above.

Pattern

Possible value: KOB, SUNSTREAK, SNOOPER, DASHER, BRINELY, SPOTTY, FLOPPER, STRIPEY, GLITTER, BLOCKFISH, BETTY, CLAYFISH.

Skull

Support 2 formats:

Base64: Like example below, only support 1.19+.

Player Name: Need online mode, support all versions. UltimateShop-Premium supports use PlaceholderAPI's placeholder here.

Firwork

Power

Firework Effect

Config section format is multi sub options.

For type possible value: BALL, BALL_LARGE, STAR, BURST, CREEPER.

Firework Star

Firework Effect

For type possible value: BALL, BALL_LARGE, STAR, BURST, CREEPER.

Suspicious Stew (1.14+)

Effects

Effects format: Potion Type ID, Duration, Amplifier, Ambient, Particles, Icon.

Bundle (1.17+)/Shulker

Contents

Config section format is slot ID and then with item format.

Brushable Blocks (1.20+)

Content

The loot item inside brushable blocks. Want custom brushable block? Consider buy our CustomArcheology plugin here!

Spawner

Only spawner option is required if you want spawner has mobs inside. Other options are optional.

Ominous Bottle (1.20.5+)

Power

Music Instrument (1.18+)

Music

Repairable

Enchantable (1.21.2+)

Glider (1.21.2+)

Item Model (1.21.2+)

Tooltip Style (1.21.2+)

Use Cooldowns (1.21.2+)

Equippable (1.21.2+) - Premium

Damage Resistant

Extra Item Format Options

Some additional ItemFormat options can be found on the Compatibility page. Their purpose is to hook with other plugins based on ItemFormat, so that other plugins can also participate in item generation.

Supported plugins at the moment:

MythicChanger

AdvancedEnchantments - Premium

NBTAPI - Premium

📋Format

📝ItemFormat™

Component Format

Paper components are configured inside the component section of an ItemFormat. This feature requires Paper 1.21.5 or later and UltimateShop Premium. Components introduced in newer Minecraft versions require the corresponding server version.

Keep material and amount at the ItemFormat root. Put Paper component options under component:

Copy

material: DIAMOND_SWORD
amount: 1

component:
name: '<aqua>Component sword'
lore:
- '<gray>Built with Paper components'
glow: true
damage: 25
max-damage: 2048

Component options are applied after the root ItemFormat options. When both configure the same property, the component value is applied last. debuild-item-method controls how items are saved to ItemFormat; you do not need to change it to build an item from a component section.

Use numbers and booleans as YAML numbers and booleans, without quotes. Use lowercase namespaced registry IDs such as minecraft:sharpness and minecraft:attack_damage. Enum values such as EPIC, EAT and ADD_NUMBER use the spelling shown in the examples.

Server version support

Minimum Paper version

Components and options

1.21.5

Base components documented below, including food, tools, equipment, consumables, damage resistance and blocking

1.21.6

Attribute display modes; equippable.can-be-sheared and equippable.shear-sound

1.21.11

Attack range, damage type, kinetic weapons, minimum attack charge, piercing weapons, swing animation and use effects

26.1

Damage resistance and blocking also accept lists of damage type IDs

26.2

Sulfur cube content

26.3

Attack/interact animations, waxed signs, cushion color, villager food, pottery patterns, mob visibility and sign text

Options for an unregistered component are not a compatibility fallback: they will not be applied on an older server. Use the examples for your server version. For a practical checklist and complete sample items, see Testing Paper Components.

The version suffix on each component heading is the minimum Paper version supported by the plugin's component adapter. Options added later have their own version heading.

Custom Name - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.name.

Sets the custom display name. MiniMessage formatting is supported.

Item Name - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.item-name.

Sets the item name component independently of the custom display name.

Lore - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.lore.

Sets the lore lines in their configured order. MiniMessage formatting is supported.

Custom Model Data - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.custom-model-data.

Each type is a separate list. float entries must be numbers and flag entries must be booleans. Values should remain in the same order and should not multiply when the item is saved and rebuilt.

Custom model data does not create a model. A resource pack must define how these values change the item's appearance.

Max Stack Size - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.max-stack.

Accepts 1–99. Use a stack size of 1 for damageable items.

Damage - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.damage.

Sets the amount of durability already used. Use a non-negative integer; this is not the remaining durability.

Max Damage - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.max-damage.

Sets the maximum durability to a positive integer. Use max-stack: 1 for a damageable item.

Enchantment Glint - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.glow.

Overrides the enchantment glint. Use true to show it or false to hide it.

Unbreakable - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.unbreakable.

Use true to prevent normal durability loss. Omit this option when testing durability loss and item breakage.

Rarity - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.rarity.

Accepts COMMON, UNCOMMON, RARE or EPIC.

Repair Cost - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.repair-cost.

Sets the prior-work penalty used by an anvil. Use a non-negative integer.

Enchantable - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.enchantable.

Sets the enchantability value. Use a non-negative integer.

Break Sound - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.break-sound.

Sets the namespaced sound key played when the item breaks. Test with a damageable item without unbreakable: true.

Enchantments - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.enchants.

Maps enchantment registry IDs to levels. These are the item’s active enchantments.

Stored Enchantments - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.stored-enchants.

Maps enchantment registry IDs to stored levels, as used by enchanted books.

Hidden Tooltip Components - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.hide-tooltip.

Lists native component registry IDs to hide in the tooltip. Hiding the tooltip does not remove the data or disable its behavior.

Attribute Modifiers - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.attributes.

Attribute type and modifier name are namespaced keys. slot uses an equipment slot group such as mainhand, offhand, hand, head, chest, legs, feet, armor or any.

Use ADD_NUMBER, ADD_SCALAR or MULTIPLY_SCALAR_1 for operation.

Attribute Display - 1.21.6+

Each entry can also use display-mode: RESET, HIDDEN or OVERRIDE. DEFAULT is an alias for RESET; TEXT is an alias for OVERRIDE. When no mode is specified, the 1.21.6+ adapter hides the modifier's tooltip display. OVERRIDE requires display-text:

Food - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.food.

Sets nutrition, saturation and whether the item can be eaten at full hunger. Use consumable to configure its use animation, duration and consume effects.

Consumables - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.consumable.

food sets nutrition and saturation. consumable controls the use animation, duration, sound and consume effects. Include can-always-eat: true when testing food at full hunger.

Consume effects are entries inside consumable.effects. Each entry has a type and its own settings:

Type

Settings

apply-effect

probability from 0.0 to 1.0, plus an effects list

play-sound

sound, a namespaced sound key

random-teleport

diameter, the teleport diameter; old chance input is also accepted

clear-effect

No additional settings; removes all status effects

A potion effect string is effect ID,duration in ticks,amplifier,ambient,particles,icon. Amplifiers start at 0. The first three fields are required; omitted boolean fields default to true. Effects are applied in the configured order. For example, put clear-effect before apply-effect if the new status effects should remain.

Each effect entry adds only its own effect. Saving a consumable uses this same numbered-entry structure.

Use Cooldown - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.use-cooldown.

cooldown-seconds accepts a non-negative number of seconds, including fractional values such as 1.5. cooldown-group is a namespaced key shared by items in the same cooldown group. Test on a usable item, for example an apple with food and consumable configured.

Use Remainder - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.use-reminder.

The existing configuration key is use-reminder. Keep that spelling even though the native component is called use_remainder. Its value is a complete nested ItemFormat, directly under use-reminder, without an extra item: wrapper. Material, amount and nested component values are preserved when saved and rebuilt.

Tool - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.tool.

Each rule string is block ID[,additional block IDs],speed,correct-for-drops. Use true, false or null for the last field. Use namespaced block IDs.

Equippable - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.equippable.

Shearing Options - 1.21.6+

These additional equipment options require Paper 1.21.6+:

Glider - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.glider.

Adds gliding behavior when true. Use an appropriate wearable item with equippable.slot: CHEST when testing.

Dyed Color - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.leather-color.

Sets the leather dye color. Accepts a quoted hex string such as '#55AAFF', an RGB string such as '85,170,255', or an RGB integer string.

Armor Trim - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.trim.

Sets armor trim material and pattern using namespaced registry IDs. Both IDs must exist on the server.

Damage Resistance - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.damage-resistant.

A string value refers to a damage type tag without a leading #, such as minecraft:is_fire.

Damage Type List - 26.1+

A list contains individual damage type IDs instead of a tag string. These are different formats: minecraft:is_fire is a tag, while minecraft:in_fire is an individual damage type.

Blocks Attacks - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.blocks-attacks.

Configures blocking behavior. block-delay-seconds is a numeric delay in seconds; block-sound is a separate namespaced sound key. A string bypassed-by value refers to a damage type tag without a leading #.

Bypassed Damage Type List - 26.1+

Use a list of individual damage type IDs for bypassed-by on Paper 26.1+.

Weapon - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.weapon.

Configures durability damage per attack and how long the weapon disables blocking.

Bundle Contents - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.bundle-contents.

Each named or numbered entry is a complete ItemFormat with material, amount and an optional component section. Saving and rebuilding must retain the nested item types, amounts and component data.

Charged Projectiles - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.charged-projectiles.

Each named or numbered child is a projectile ItemFormat. Do not put a single material directly under charged-projectiles. A saved and rebuilt crossbow should retain its configured projectile.

Banner Patterns - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.banner-patterns.

Each list entry uses pattern registry ID: DYE_COLOR. Patterns are applied in the configured order.

Potion Contents - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.potion.

base-effect uses a PotionType enum value. Custom effects use effect ID,duration in ticks,amplifier,ambient,particles,icon; the effect ID is namespaced and amplifiers start at 0.

Potion Duration Scale - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.potion-duration-scale.

Multiplies the duration of potion effects. Test on an item with potion contents.

Suspicious Stew Effects - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.suspicious-stew-effects.

Each entry needs only effect ID,duration in ticks. Older entries with additional fields remain readable; those extra fields do not change the stew effect.

Fireworks - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.firework.

Use trail, not trial. Flight duration is inside firework, alongside the numbered effect entries; it accepts 0–255.

Axolotl Variant - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.axolotl-variant.

Sets the axolotl bucket variant using an enum value such as BLUE or LUCY. Test with AXOLOTL_BUCKET.

Tropical Fish Base Color - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.tropical-fish-base-color.

Sets the tropical fish bucket’s body dye color. Test with TROPICAL_FISH_BUCKET.

Tropical Fish Pattern Color - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.tropical-fish-pattern-color.

Sets the tropical fish bucket’s pattern dye color. Test with TROPICAL_FISH_BUCKET.

Tropical Fish Pattern - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.tropical-fish-pattern.

Sets the tropical fish bucket’s pattern enum, such as KOB.

Player Profile - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.skull.

skull (or skull-meta) is a base64 texture property in this Paper component format, rather than a player-name lookup. Texture display also depends on the client being able to load the texture. Test with PLAYER_HEAD.

Instrument - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.music.

Sets a registered instrument using its namespaced ID. Test with GOAT_HORN.

Jukebox Playable - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.song.

Sets a registered jukebox song using its namespaced ID. Test by inserting the item into a jukebox.

Ominous Bottle Amplifier - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.ominous-bottle-amplifier.

Accepts 0–4. Test with OMINOUS_BOTTLE.

Item Model - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.item-model.

Sets the namespaced item model key. Custom models require matching resource pack assets; the key alone does not create a model.

Tooltip Style - 1.21.5+

Configuration key: component.tooltip-style.

Sets a custom tooltip style key. The spelling is tooltip-style, not tootip-style. A matching resource pack asset is required to change its appearance.

Attack Range - 1.21.11+

Configuration key: component.attack-range-key.

The configuration key is attack-range-key. Reach values accept 0–64, hitbox-margin accepts 0–1, and mob-factor accepts 0–2.

Damage Type - 1.21.11+

Configuration key: component.damage-type.

Sets the weapon’s damage type using an individual damage type registry ID, rather than a damage type tag.

Kinetic Weapon - 1.21.11+

Configuration key: component.kinetic-weapon.

Configures kinetic weapon contact cooldown, delay, movement, damage multiplier, sounds and conditions. Condition durations are measured in ticks. Test separately from other components that change the same attack interaction.

Minimum Attack Charge - 1.21.11+

Configuration key: component.minimum-attack-charge.

Accepts a value from 0 to 1 for the minimum attack charge.

Piercing Weapon - 1.21.11+

Configuration key: component.piercing-weapon.

Configures piercing weapon knockback, dismount behavior and sounds.

Swing Animation - 1.21.11+

Configuration key: component.swing-animation.

Configures the legacy swing animation. On Paper 1.21.11 through 26.2 this supplies the swing component. On Paper 26.3+ it supplies both attack and interact animations, except where an explicit attack-animation or interact-animation section overrides its respective side.

Use Effects - 1.21.11+

Configuration key: component.use-effects.

Configures sprinting, interaction vibrations and movement speed while using the item. speed-multiplier accepts 0–1. Test on a usable item with a use duration, for example an apple with food and consumable configured.

Sulfur Cube Content - 26.2+

Configuration key: component.sulfur-cube-content.

The content is a complete nested ItemFormat.

Attack Animation - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.attack-animation.

Sets the attack animation independently. Takes precedence over swing-animation for attack behavior.

Interact Animation - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.interact-animation.

Sets the interaction animation independently. Takes precedence over swing-animation for interaction behavior.

Sign Text Front - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.sign-text-front.

Configures the front side of a sign. Use four lines, a dye color and glowing as needed. Test by placing an OAK_SIGN item.

Sign Text Back - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.sign-text-back.

Configures the back side of a sign. Use four lines, a dye color and glowing as needed. Test by placing an OAK_SIGN item.

Waxed - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.waxed.

Use true to add the waxed state to a sign item. Check whether the placed sign is sealed against editing.

Cushion Color - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.cushion-color.

Sets the cushion dye color using a dye color enum such as RED. Use an appropriate material and interaction to check the visible result.

Villager Food - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.villager-food.

Sets the item’s villager food value. Test the relevant villager interaction with an appropriate item material.

Provides Pottery Pattern - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.provides-pottery-pattern.

Sets a decorated pot pattern using its namespaced registry ID. The pattern must exist on the server.

Mob Visibility - 26.3+

Configuration key: component.mob-visibility.

Applies a visibility factor to the listed entity types. visibility accepts 0–10. Entity IDs must exist in the server registry.

Saving and testing

Set debuild-item-method: COMPONENT in config.yml and reload the plugin to save supported item properties inside a component section. This setting does not change how Minecraft stores the item itself.

For file installation, give commands, round-trip checks and version-specific examples, continue to Testing Paper Components.

📋Format

➗Math Calculate Format

Unlike most plugins, UltimateShop allows you to fill in the Placeholder API and mathematical calculation format in almost all options applicable to numbers, making it easy to set the price, buy/sell limits, and more you want.

You need enable math.enabled option in your config.yml file to use this feature.

Result precision

math.scale controls the number of decimal places in mathematical results. UltimateShop uses HALF_UP rounding. Applying a multiplier also counts as a mathematical calculation, so multiplier results—including sell price modifiers, previews, and final rewards—use the same scale.

Copy

math:
scale: 2

For example, with scale: 2, multiplying 10 by 0.333333 produces 3.33.

List of match calculate format

() - Create a parenthetical expression which will be evaluated first (3 * (4 + 1))

$ - Denotes a variable ($1 / 3)

e - Euler's constant (log(e))

pi - pi (sin(pi))

+ - Add two numbers (1 + 1)

- - Subtract two numbers, or negate one (3-2, -(4+2))

/ - Divide two numbers (3 / 4)

* - Multiply two numbers (2 * 3)

^ - Raise one number to the power of another (3^3)

% - Take the modulus, or division remainder, of one number with another (7 % 4)

abs - Take the absolute value of a number (abs$1, abs-1)

round - Rounds a number to the nearest integer (round1.5, round(2.3))

ceil - Rounds a number up to the nearest integer (ceil1.05)

floor - Rounds a number down to the nearest integer (floor0.95)

rand - Generate a random number between 0 and the specified upper bound (rand4)

log - Get the natural logarithm of a number (log(e))

sqrt - Get the square root of a number (sqrt4)

cbrt - Get the cube root of a number (cbrt(8))

sin - Get the sine of a number (sin$2)

cos - Get the cosine of a number (cos(2*pi))

tan - Get the tangent of a number (tanpi)

asin - Get the arcsine of a number (asin$2)

acos - Get the arccosine of a number (acos0.45)

atan - Get the arctangent of a number (atan1)

sinh - Get the hyperbolic sine of a number (sinh(4))

cosh - Get the hyperbolic cosine of a number (sinh(4))

true - Boolean constant representing 1

false - Boolean constant representing 0

= - Compare if two numbers are equal (1 = 1 will be 1, 1 = 3 will be 0), also accepts ==

!= - Compare if two numbers are not equal (1 != 2 will be 1, 1 != 1 will be 0)

> - Compare if one number is greater than another (1 > 0)

< - Compare if one number is less than another (0 < 1)

>= - Compare if one number is greater than or equal to another (1 >= 1)

<= - Compare if one number is less than or equal to another (0 <= 1)

| - Boolean or (true | false), also accepts ||

& - Boolean and (true & true), also accepts &&

! - Boolean not/inverse (!true)

Functions

Every function in this table, including piecewise(), requires both math.enabled: true and math.enable-function: true.

Function

What it returns

Example

sum(...)

The sum of all arguments

sum(1, 2, 3) → 6

max(...)

The largest argument

max(5, 12, 8) → 12

min(...)

The smallest argument

min(5, 12, 8) → 5

avg(...)

The arithmetic average

avg(10, 20, 30) → 20

random(...)

A random decimal in the given range

random(2, 5) → at least 2 and less than 5

piecewise(...)

The value for the first true condition

piecewise(3 < 5, 10, 20) → 10

sum, max, min, and avg return 0 when called without arguments.

Random numbers

random() returns a decimal from 0 up to 1.

random(4) uses the range from 0 up to 4. With a negative argument, such as random(-4), the range runs from -4 up to 0.

random(2, 5) uses the range from 2 up to 5. You can give the endpoints in either order. Extra arguments after the first two are ignored.

The upper endpoint is excluded. Equal endpoints return that value, so random(0) returns 0 and random(5, 5) returns 5.

Piecewise calculations

piecewise(condition1, value1, condition2, value2, fallback) returns the value for the first true condition or the fallback. For example, piecewise(3 < 5, 10, 20) returns 10. The engine evaluates all arguments before choosing the result, so every branch expression must be valid.

For a practical example using min, max, log, and floor together, see the Dynamic Price YAML.

📋Format

📊Understanding YAML/This wiki

In the UltimateShop plugin, all configuration files use the YAML format. Understanding YAML syntax is essential for editing and customizing your setup. Let’s go through it with real examples from the plugin.

Basic Syntax

The basic structure of YAML is key: value. Example:

Copy

debug: false

Important Rules:

Keys are case-sensitive.

There must be a space after the colon.

When the value contains special characters (: or &), wrap it in quotes: prefix: "&a[UltimateShop]"

When the value contains a single quote ('), escape it with two single quotes: message: "You can''t buy this item!"

Hierarchy & Indentation

Indentation represents parent-child relationships in YAML. Each level must be indented by two spaces. Example:

Copy

auto-save:
hide-message: false

Here:

auto-save is the parent key.

hide-message is its child key, means this option only work for auto save feature.

false is the value, means we will not hide message when auto save.

Let us take shop configs as complex example:

1️⃣ Level 1 (Top Level) There are two main root keys:

settings

items

They exist side by side at the top of the configuration. This is shop configs, so you should get available top level options at Shops page. In that page, we told you available keys with very big font size.

with very detalied example.

All of them has detelied info:

2️⃣ Level 2 Under settings:

menu

buy-more

shop-name

hide-message
are all child keys of settings.

This means all of them are settings for this shop. Put them in other place will NOT work.

Under items:

A is a child key representing a product. A means this product ID is A, and all section under A will only work for this product.

3️⃣ Level 3 Under items → A:

price-mode

product-mode

products

buy-prices

and so on.
are all children of A. All of those options will only work for product A, and should not put them in other place.

Since it is product config, you can get all options available at Products page with detalied info.

4️⃣ Level 4 Under products:

“1” means the first defined product.

Under buy-prices:

“1” means the first defined price set.

You can set unlimited sub price and product. All of those options available at Single Things page.

5️⃣ Level 5 Under products → 1:

material: GRASS_BLOCK → the item type.

amount: 1 → the quantity.

They are following ItemFormat.

Under buy-prices → 1:

economy-plugin: Vault → the economy system used.

amount: '0.63' → the price.

placeholder: '{amount}$' → how the price is displayed.

They are following EconomyFormat and placeholder option inside price section.

Hierarchical Tree Diagram

Lists

When a key holds multiple values, use a list.

Example:

Each “-” must be indented two spaces from its parent key.

Empty and Default Values

An empty list is represented as:

The [] means the list is empty, and the plugin will use its default settings.
This line can usually be safely removed.

Previous🆚Compare

Next🛒Info of ItemFormat™

Last updated 4 months ago

Basic Syntax

Hierarchy & Indentation

Hierarchical Tree Diagram

Lists

Empty and Default Values

Copy

settings:
menu: 'example-shop-menu'
buy-more: true
shop-name: 'Blocks Shop'
hide-message: false

items:
A:
price-mode: CLASSIC_ALL
product-mode: CLASSIC_ALL
products:
1:
material: GRASS_BLOCK
amount: 1
buy-prices:
1:
economy-plugin: Vault
amount: '0.63'
placeholder: '{amount}$'

Copy

items:
buy-prices: # Will not work
1:
economy-plugin: Vault
amount: '0.63'
placeholder: '{amount}$'
A:
menu: 'example-shop-menu' # Will not work.
buy-more: true # Will not work.
shop-name: 'Blocks Shop' # Will not work.
hide-message: false # Will not work.
price-mode: CLASSIC_ALL # Only work for product A
product-mode: CLASSIC_ALL # Only work for product A
products: # Only work for product A
1:
material: GRASS_BLOCK
amount: 1

Copy

items: # Will work.
B: # Will work.
products: # Will work.
1: # Will work.
material: APPLE # Will work.
amount: 64 # Will work.
give-actions: # Will work.
1: # Will work.
multi-once: true # Will work.
type: message # Will work.
message: 'eco give {player} {amount}' # Will work.

Copy

settings
│
├─ menu
├─ buy-more
├─ shop-name
└─ hide-message

items
└─ A
├─ price-mode
├─ product-mode
├─ products
│ └─ 1
│ ├─ material
│ └─ amount
└─ buy-prices
└─ 1
├─ economy-plugin
├─ amount
└─ placeholder

Copy

menu:
secret-shop-items:
- diamond_sword
- netherite_pickaxe
- enchanted_golden_apple

Copy

menu:
secret-shop-items: []

📦Info

⌨️Commands & Permissions

FAQ:

Does the /shop command has permission?

A: Use /shop can directly open a menu called main. This is a feature called Auto Open, and you can disable it at config.yml file with menu.auto-open.enabled option. If you only want some players to be exposed to this command, you can set conditions for the menu so that only players who meet the specified conditions can open the menu. For more info, please view Menus page.

Why it say condition not meet when open daily example menu?

A: That menu already has a condition, which you can find in menus/shop_menu_templates/daily-shop-menu.yml.

ultimateshop.bypassprice

This permission can help you bypass price check and won't cost any price.

ultimateshop.bypass.protection

This permission can help you bypass protection (anti grief) check when using sell stick.

/shop menu <menuID>/<shopID>

Open common menu or shop menu.

Require ultimateshop.menu permission.

For console, should extra add <player> arg at the end of command, like /shop menu test Player1.

Support add -b at the end of the command to bypass menu open condition check. (Premium version only)

/shop quickbuy <shopID> <productID> [amount]

Quick purchase item.

Require ultimateshop.quickbuy permission.

For console, should extra add <player> arg at the end of command, like /shop quickbuy test A Player1.

/shop quicksell <shopID> <productID> [amount]

Same as quickbuy, just replace quickbuy to quicksell.

amount arg can be replaced to * symbol, then plugin will auto sell all the items you can sell. (Premium version only)

If the corresponding product is not displayed in the shop GUI, or if the player does not meet the conditions to open the corresponding shop menu, the item cannot be traded. If this is not desired, change the settings.secret-shop-items option in the shop configuration to false.

/shop reload

Reload the plugin, some configs need you restart server.

Require ultimateshop.reload permission.

/shop givesellstick <itemID> <playerID> [amount] (Premium version only)

Give specifeid player specified amount (if not set, default to 1) sell stick.

Require ultimateshop.givesellstick permission.

/shop givesellchest <itemID> <playerID> [amount] (Premium version only)

Give specifeid player specified amount (if not set, default to 1) sell chest.

Require ultimateshop.givesellstick permission.

/shop setbuytimes/setselltimes <shopID> <productID> <player>/global [times]

Set player's specified product buy times to specified value.

Require ultimateshop.setbuytimes permission.

If didn't set times arg, we will think you are trying to reset the buy/sell times.

productID arg can be replaced to * symbol, then plugin will auto pick up all product in specified shop. (Premium version only)

setselltimes is similar to setbuytimes here.

The global arg means set buy/sell times for {buy-times-server} or {sell-times-server} placeholer, not means set buy/sell times for all players.

It is impossible to set all player data at once through commands in UltimateShop. Because assuming your server has hundreds of thousands of player data, without excellent performance optimization code, the server will immediately crash. You may see very few economy plugins or item plugins providing this feature, but they are selling it as a selling point. We have not declared ourselves providing this feature on any occasion, and this feature will not be added in the future because it is very time-consuming and not very meaningful. You can achieve similar functions through the auto reset function, and relevant information can be viewed on this page.

/shop addbuytimes/addselltimes <shopID> <productID> <player>/global <times>

Add specified value to player's specified product buy times.

Require ultimateshop.addtbuytimes permission.

productID arg can be replaced to * symbol, then plugin will auto pick up all product in specified shop. (Premium version only)

addselltimes is similar to setbuytimes here.

/shop sellall

Open sellall menu.

Require ultimateshop.sellall permission.

/shop saveitem <saveItemID> <saveItemMethod>

Save your hold items. For more info, please view Save Item page.

Require ultimateshop.saveitem permission.

/shop givesaveitem [player] [amount]

Give a saved item from plugins/UltimateShop/items/. The ID is the file name without .yml. In game, /shop givesaveitem component-test gives the item to yourself; /shop givesaveitem component-test PlayerName 1 targets an online player. The console must specify a player.

Require ultimateshop.givesaveitem permission. See Saved Item for creating and reloading ItemFormat files.

/shop generateitemformat

Generate the item held in your main hand into plugins/UltimateShop/generated-item-format.yml. Each use overwrites this file. debuild-item-method: COMPONENT saves supported properties inside component on Paper 1.21.5+ with UltimateShop Premium. See Testing Paper Components for a save/rebuild walkthrough.

Require ultimateshop.generateitemformat permission.

/shop getplaceholdervalue <text>

Parse input text to get placeholder value in it.

Require ultimateshop.getplaceholdervalue permission.

/shop resetrandomplaceholder <placeholderID> [player] (Premium version only)

Reset random placeholder value.

The per player's random placeholder must enter the player name in the command parameters, while the none-per player's random placeholder cannot enter the player name in the command parameters, otherwise the plugin will prompt an error.

Require ultimateshop.resetrandomplaceholder permission.

/shop setrandomplaceholder <placeholderID> [element] [player] (Premium version only)

Set random placeholder value.

Different from resetrandomplaceholder, setrandomplaceholder command won't reset refresh time and allow users pick specifeid element.

The per player's random placeholder must enter the player name in the command parameters, while the per player's random placeholder cannot enter the player name in the command parameters, otherwise the plugin will prompt an error.

Support add -b at the end of the command to bypass element exist check, which means you can set the custom element you'd like. For example, my random placeholder only have A,B,C total 3 elements, if I type D as element here, plugin will print error message, if you add -b suffix, then the placeholder value will be set to D and plugin never print error message, but it is not recommended. (Removed in 3.12.0, now this command no longer check element exist)

Require ultimateshop.setrandomplaceholder permission.

/shop search

Print the message that helps you know the hold item target product in shop.

Require ultimateshop.search permission.

/shop sellhand

Sell the entire stack in your main hand only.

Require ultimateshop.sellhand permission.

/shop sellallhand

Sell all hand item.

Require ultimateshop.sellallhand permission.

sellhand = sell only the held stack.
sellallhand = use the held item as selector, then sell matching items from inventory.

/shop updategui [player]

Update GUI for specified player. Only buttons will be updated, inventory title will not update.

Require ultimateshop.updategui permission.

/shop updateguititle [player] (Premium version only)

Update GUI title for specifed player. Only gui title will be updated, buttons will not update.

Require server enable title update feature, for more info, please view this page.

Require ultimateshop.updateguititle permission.

/shop editor (Premium version only)

Open the built-in in-game editor. Set editor.enabled: true in config.yml and reload the plugin to enable it. Back up the plugin configuration before every use. No separate editor addon is needed.

Use /shop editor <scope> <id> to open a file directly. Scopes are shop, menu, shop_menu_template, buy_more_menu, favourite_menu, search_menu and item_sell_menu. IDs omit .yml; specialized menu scopes use their matching folders under menus/. See In-game Editor for UI presets and saving changes.

Require ultimateshop.editor permission.

Custom Placeholder Commands

For command list of custom placeholder feature, please view this page.

📦Info

🆚Compare

Free

Premium

More Commands
Some commands are only available for premium version. For more info, please view Commands page.

❌

✅

More Command Args
Premium version supports more command args. Like use * means all products. For more info, please view Commands page.

❌

✅

Bedrock Menus
Premium version automatically translates Java menus into Bedrock Form UI and supports automatic vanilla item icons and manually configured custom icons. For more info, please view Bedrock Menus page.

❌

✅

Dialog Menus
Premium version supports Minecraft's native Dialog UI on Paper 1.21.9 or newer for Java clients. Reuse existing menu files with action buttons or the item-action-list layout, including item icons and clickable labels. These layouts do not require a resource pack. For more info, please view Dialog Menus page.

❌

✅

Ore Resource-Pack Menus
Premium version supports a graphical Dialog layout with custom backgrounds, buttons and product cards. Product cards display an item icon beside the product name and lore, with a details button for buying and selling. Customize textures and layout templates while reusing existing menu conditions and actions. Requires Paper 1.21.9 or newer, Java clients and the generated resource pack. For more info, please view Ore Resource-Pack Layout page.

❌

✅

Multi Server Sync
Premium version supports sync global server's data with multi server in one proxy server. For more info, please view Multi Server Sync page.

❌

✅

Localized Item Name
Premium version supports display item localized name for item without custom name. For more info, please view Localized Item Name page.

❌

✅

Random Placeholder
Premium version supports use built-in random placeholder. For more info, please view Random Placeholders page.

❌

✅

Conditional Placeholder
Premium version supports use built-in conditional placeholder. For more info, please view Coditional Placeholders page.

❌

✅

Custom Placeholder (Beta)
Supports custom numeric or text values, stored separately for each player or shared globally. The free version supports up to 5 custom placeholders, the premium version has no limit. Use commands to set values or add to numeric values, then display them with custom placeholders. For more info, please view Custom Placeholder page.

✅ Up to 5

✅ No limit

Dynamic Price Status
Premium version allows you compare the now price and the base price of dynamic price and display the status at product info lore. For more info, please view Dynamic Price Status page.

❌

✅

Sell Multiplier
Premium version supports conditional sell bonuses or taxes. Choose the highest matching multiplier with MAX, or multiply matching entries together with STACK. For more info, please view Sell Multiplier page.

❌

✅

Price Modifier (Experimental)
Adjust sell rewards according to the actual item being sold, including durability, numeric values from lore or NBT, and MythicChanger matching rules. Enable it per product with price-modifier: true. This feature is experimental; some modifier types require additional plugins. For more info, please view Price Modifier page.

✅

✅

More Built-in Placeholders
Premium version supports more built-in placeholders. For more info, please view Built-in Placeholders page.

❌

✅

More Advanced Actions
Premium version supports use more advanced actions. For more info, please view Action Format page.

❌

✅

More Advanced Conditions
Premium version supports use more advanced conditions. For more info, please view Condition Format page.

❌

✅

More Advanced Reset Mode
Premium version supports use more advanced product buy times or sell times reset mode. For more info, please view Buy/Sell Times Reset page.

❌

✅

Sell Stick
Premium version allows you create and use sell stick to quickly sell all contents from the container. For more info, please view Sell Stick page.

❌

✅

Sell Chest (Experimental)
Premium version supports sell chests that automatically sell their contents while the chunk is loaded and the owner is online. Configure usage limits, sell multipliers and hologram information. Folia is not supported. For more info, please view Sell Chest page.

❌

✅

More ItemFormat™ Key Support
Premium version supports additional ItemFormat™ options, including AdvancedEnchantments integration, Data Components on Paper 1.21.6 or newer, and Equippable settings on Minecraft 1.21.2 or newer. For more info, please view ItemFormat page.

❌

✅

Log Transaction
Premium version allows you use this feature to log all translation info, you can easily check things like whether your price settings are working correctly as you want. For more info, please view Log Transaction page.

❌

✅

Custom Click Event
Premium version allows you to customize what happens when players click on products with different buttons. For more info, please view Custom Click Event page.

❌

✅

Custom Single Thing Type
Premium version allows you use CUSTOM single thing type. For more info, please view Single Thing page.

❌

✅

Sub Button for Products
This feature allows you display same product at multi slot or menus, even support display different item at different slot or menu, but, they are still same product. For more info, please view Products page.

❌

✅

Dynamic Layout
Premium version users can use dynamic values in menu layout. For more info, please view General Menus page.

❌

✅

Conditional Display
Premium version supports displaying different products or buttons in the same menu slot according to conditions. Entries are checked in order, allowing fallback choices when a condition is not met. For more info, please view Conditional Display page.

❌

✅

Custom Command
Premium version users can add custom command to open common menus or shop menus. For more info, please view General Menus page.

❌

✅

Auto Update GUI Title
Premium version supports auto update GUI title after each click or each seconds. For more info, please view General Menus page.

❌

✅

Per Player Language & Advanced Language Message Format (Support send boss bar, action bar, sound, title in message!)
Click here to know more.

❌

✅

Shulker Box Sell (Alpha)
After enable this feature in config.yml file, plugin will auto check content of shulker box and sell them. Should support common sell, sell stick, sell all GUI, sell chest and so on.

❌

✅

In-game Editor
Open the in-game editor with /shop editor to edit shops, products and menus. For more info, please view In-game Editor page.

❌

✅

Search GUI
You can search product you want in Search GUI. For more info, please view this page.

❌

✅

Favourite Menus
Premium version allows players to save favourite products and access them from a dedicated menu. Players can reorder or remove saved products in edit mode. For more info, please view Favourite Menus page.

❌

✅

Shared Product Data (Alpha)
You can set multi product share same product data like buy times/sell times/limits/cooldown reset time and so on. For more info, please view this page.

❌

✅

More...
Many small differences were not mentioned here or, I forgot them put here, and more features that are only available in the paid version will be added in the future!

❌
