📦Info

🔗Compatibility

The compatibility of plugins mainly includes hooks for item plugins and economy plugins. Unlike other plugins of the same type, we can support them regardless of their appearance. There are two methods to support them: direct compatibility and indirect compatibility.

Direct compatibility

Direct compatibility refers to the use of item plugins or the economy of economic plugins directly in ItemFormat or EconomyFormat. This compatibility method is the simplest and officially supported.

Directly supported item plugins list

ItemsAdder

Oraxen

EcoItems

EcoArmor

MMOItems

MythicMobs

eco

NeigeItems

ExecutableItems

Nexo

CraftEngine

You can use ItemBridge as custom item provider which supports more custom item plugins, click here to know more.

Directly supported economy plugins list

PlayerPoints

CoinsEngine

UltraEconomy

EcoBits

PEconomy

RedisEconomy

RoyaleEconomy

VotingPlugin

The following provides an example of directly obtaining items from the ItemsAdder plugin through the direct compatibility feature in ItemFormat and using economy from Vault plugin in EconomyFormat:

Directly supported protection plugins list

Although the protection plugin you are using is not on this list, as long as the corresponding plugin can prevent the player from interacting with the corresponding container, UltimateShop will not trigger a sell stick, depending on how the author of the plugin you are using wrote their plugin.

If players do not have permission to open container within these protection plugins areas, UltimateShop can prevent players use sell stick in these areas.

BentoBox

Dominion

GriefPrevention

HuskTowns

HuskClaims

Lands

PlotSquared

Residence

Towny

WorldGuard

SuperiorSkyblock2

Directly supported hologram plugins list

Support create hologram to display sell chest info.

CMI

DecentHolograms

FancyHolograms

Indirect compatibility

Indirect compatibility refers to the flexible use of various features of plugins to enable them to associate with these plugins.

Save Item: We told you a command called /shop saveitem at Commands page, we also told you can set material option in Item Format to the save item ID you was set to use them.

Buy Actions: We told you a option called buy-actions in shop configs at Shops page. In Actions page, we also told you we support use command in actions, so just use the give item command here, all is done.

Give Actions: We told you this feature at Single Things page which is very similar to Buy Actions. What's more, we even give you an example at that page.

Example: Use for not supported item plugins as products

In this example, we first fill in the ItemFormat through the display item option to describe the item from an incompatible plugin, so that players can see what the item looks like in the menu.

In the products option, we use the Custom Sell Match feature, which allows us to flexibly set the rules for selling matches for this item, such as contains-lore, etc. Then, we use give-actions format to execute the item give command, so that player can obtain this item after buy.

If you use the Paper server and the item is fixed (the items generated each time are identical), you can use the Save item function: you only need to use the /shop saveitem command, then use the material option in ItemFormat, and fill in the ID of the save item in this option.

Example: Use for not supported economy plugins as prices.

In this example, we mainly flexibly implemented different types of single thing and give-actions and take-actions options, whose functions can be found on the Products page. Specifically, assuming the player purchases this product, the match-placeholder in buy options is used to determine if the player has enough economy. If it meets the buy-prices requirement, the player will receive an apple in products section and execute take-actions section in buy-prices section. Similarly, during selling, as the player obtains the sell price, the give-actions in the sell price will be executed, and therefore the player will receive economy.

Require PREMIUM version of UltimateShop!

MythicChanger: Extra Item Format option

Through this hook, based on the ItemFormat, items can be further modified to their desired appearance.

This feature require your server must install MythicChanger plugin, please get it here:

FREE: Click to download

PREMIUM: Click to download

For how to configure the change-item section, please read MythicChanger's wiki, click here to visit. Please note that some of the change rules require PREMIUM version of MythicChanger, not PREMIUM version of UltimateShop!

AdvancedEnchantments: Extra Item Format option - Premium

Through this hook, based on the ItemFormat, items can have custom enchantments from AdvancedmentEnchantments.

Plugin like EcoEnchants, ExcellentEnchants are vanilla enchants like plugin, you just need to put their enchantment ID to enchants option in ItemFormat.

You can use plugin-enchants option to add plugin enchants for your item.

NBTAPI: Extra Item Format option - Premium

The format of this option is:

Supported NBT Type: '

byte

short

int

long

float

double

string

For example:

MythicChanger: Custom Item Match

Custom Item Match Method feature requires MythicChanger.

PlaceholderAPI: Extra placeholders

UltimateShop provides those new placeholders to PlaceholderAPI, for more info, please view this page.

Indirect compatibility

Example: Use for not supported item plugins as products

Example: Use for not supported economy plugins as prices.

MythicChanger: Extra Item Format option

AdvancedEnchantments: Extra Item Format option - Premium

NBTAPI: Extra Item Format option - Premium

MythicChanger: Custom Item Match

PlaceholderAPI: Extra placeholders

Copy

items:
A:
price-mode: CLASSIC_ALL
product-mode: CLASSIC_ALL
products:
1:
hook-plugin: ItemsAdder # Item Format
hook-item: fishing_pack:common_fishing_bait # Item Forma
buy-prices:
1:
economy-plugin: Vault # Economy Format
amount: 5
start-apply: 0
placeholder: '&65 Coins'

Copy

display-item:
material: APPLE
# You can hold the item and type command /shop generateitemformat to get the ItemFormat here.
products:
1:
# Sell Match
match-item:
contains-lore:
- 'test1'
# Buy Give Command
give-actions:
1:
multi-once: true
type: console_command
command: 'items give {player} {amount}'
2:
type: message
message: 'test message'
amount: 64

Copy

products:
1:
# The product
material: APPLE
amount: 64
buy-prices:
1:
# Buy Match Placeholder
match-placeholder: '%economy_now_balance_placeholder%'
amount: 500
# Buy Take Actions
take-actions:
1:
multi-once: true
type: console_command
command: 'eco take {player} {amount}'
sell-prices:
1:
# Sell Give Actions
give-actions:
1:
multi-once: true
type: 'console_command'
command: 'eco give {player} {amount}'
amount: 500

Copy

change-item:
set-name: '&fGood Diamond Sword'

Copy

plugin-enchants:
PLANTER: 5 # A AdvancedEnchantments enchantment

Copy

nbt:
<NBT Type>:
<NBT Key>: <NBT Value>

Copy

nbt:
string:
customNBT: 'Hello!'
int:
anotherNBTComponent.theNBTKey: 5

📦Info

🛠️Configuration files

The plugin generates the following configuration files, some of which will only be generated after you first use this feature.

datas: The location for storing plugin data files. It will only be generated without using a database. Do not modify any content here.

items: The location for storing saved item files.

languages: The location for storing language files. You can set the language file used by the plugin through the config-files.language option in the config.yml file. You can customize various messages within the plugin game through language files. For per player language, or send action bar/title/boss bar/sound, please view this page.

menus/common_menus: The location for storing common menu configuration files.

menus/shop_menu_templates: The location for storing shop menu template files.

ui: Shared graphical style files (classic.yml, bedrock.yml and custom presets). Select them with menu.ui.style or menu ui.style; see Menu Configuration & Inheritance.

menus/buy_more_menus: The location for storing buy more menu files.

menus/favourite_menus: The location for storing favourite menu files.

menus/search_menus: The location for storing search menu files.

menus/item_sell_menus: The location for storing item sell menu files.

Many people seem to be confused about one thing: what you open through the /shop command is a menu, not a shop. If you want to set the slot for items in the shop menu, you should set it in the configuration file of the menu.

shops: The location for storing shop configuration files.

placeholders/random_placeholders: The loaction for storing random placeholder configuration files.

placeholders/custom_placeholders: The location for storing custom placeholder configuration files. The free version loads up to 5.

placeholders/conditional_placeholders: The location for storing conditional placeholder configuration files.

sell_sticks: The location for storing sell stick configuration files.

config.yml file: The location for main common settings for plugins.

generated-item-format.yml file: When using the /shop generateitemformat command, we will parse the item you are holding into an ItemFormat and store the parsed ItemFormat content in this file.

minecraft-resources/<version>/: Downloaded Minecraft data, textures and language caches used by automatic menu icons and localized item names. These files are prepared automatically.

XX.txt file: The transaction record file generated by the Log Transaction feature is named using the log-transaction.file option in the config.yml file.

Legacy top-level placeholder and specialized menu folders are automatically moved into placeholders/ and menus/ when the plugin starts. If both locations contain the same file, the new file is used and the old file is preserved with a console warning for manual merging. Existing common menu files and custom subfolders in menus/ are moved into menus/common_menus/.

For Paper component ItemFormat examples and save/rebuild checks, see Component Format and Testing Paper Components.

Config.yml file content

It is recommend that you view this file at GitHub, becuase Wiki's config.yml maybe not latest. Click here to view this file on Github.

Last updated 17 hours ago

Copy

# UltimateShop by @PQguanfang
#
# Read the Wiki: ultimateshop.superiormc.cn

# Some options require restart the server to make effect.

debug: false

config-files:
language: en_US
# Premium version only.
per-player-language: true
force-parse-mini-message: true

# Support value: COMPONENT or LEGACY
# COMPONENT mode requires Paper 1.21.5+ and UltimateShop Premium.
debuild-item-method: 'LEGACY'

# Support value: DEFAULT or ITEMBRIDGE
hook-item-method: 'DEFAULT'

# By default, we will auto force display fail message to make sure player know whether they have successfully clicked the product button.
# If set to false, we will not force display fail message and you can also use hide-message option in shop configs to hide the fail messages.
force-display-fail-message: false

cache:
# If you are facing multi servers sync issue, try greater this value.
load-delay: 7
reset-task:
# Reset deadlines are rounded forward into this many seconds so nearby entries run together.
# Set to 0 to disable deadline merging. Maximum value: 3600.
merge-seconds: 1
# Maximum number of due reset entries processed in one server tick. Maximum value: 10000.
max-tasks-per-tick: 100

sell:
# Support Value: Bukkit or ItemFormat.
# For each product, you can add match-item section to make custom sell match method, for more info, please view Wiki.
sell-method: Bukkit
# Only support ItemFormat sell method.
item-format:
require-same-key: false
ignore-key:
- 'lore'
- 'damage'
- 'enchants'
- 'tool.damage-per-block'
- 'nbt.string.CustomNBTKey'
auto-hide-sell-all-message: true
sell-all:
ignore-items:
# Format: Shop ID;;Item ID
- 'hideshop;;A'
# PREMIUM version only.
sell-stick:
# The min value here is 5, if you set value less than 5, we will auto set the cooldown to 5 tick.
# In ticks.
cooldown: 5
# Support value: LEFT, RIGHT and LEFT;;RIGHT
# Do not change this value unless you are know what you are doing!
click-type: RIGHT
# PREMIUM version only.
sell-chest:
enabled: true
debug: false
period-ticks: 600
batch-count: 5
send-sell-message: true
price-empty: '{lang}'
hologram:
enabled: true
# Support value: DecentHolograms, CMI, FancyHolograms
plugin: 'DecentHolograms'
max-amount: -1
max-search-attempts: 5
# Premium version only.
shulker-box-sell: true
# Premium version only
multiplier:
enabled: false
display-original-price: true
black-dynamic-price: true
black-shops:
- 'example'
# Support value: MAX, STACK
# MAX mode: will use the maximum value as the result
# STACK mode: As long as the player meets the conditions, it will be stacked and multiplied.
mode: STACK
value:
default: 1
rich: 0.9
vip: 1.1
value-conditions:
# Tax
rich:
1:
type: placeholder
placeholder: '%vault_eco_balance%'
rule: '>='
value: 50000
# Bonus for VIP
vip:
1:
type: permission
permission: 'group.vip'
# Item-aware modifiers are a separate global sell feature.
# Modifiers are applied in configuration order and merged into the final multiplier.
price-modifier:
item-sell-menu:
# Products with `price-modifier: true` open this item sell menu when a sell action is chosen.
enabled: true
menu: item-sell
durability:
type: durability
enabled: true
# 1 means losing 1% durability deducts 1% from the price.
deduction-coefficient: 1
minimum-multiplier: 0.1
# You can use minimum-price instead of, or together with, minimum-multiplier.
# minimum-price: 1
lore:
type: lore
enabled: false
# SET uses the captured number as the price; MULTIPLY multiplies the current price by it.
# Invalid operations are reported and the modifier is ignored.
operation: SET
# The default pattern matches lore such as: Item Value: 500
pattern: 'Item Value[：:]\s*([+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)?|\.\d+))'
value-group: 1
strip-color: true
case-sensitive: true
# Values outside this inclusive range, or longer than maximum-number-length, are ignored.
minimum-value: 0
maximum-value: 1000000
maximum-number-length: 64
nbt:
type: nbt
enabled: false
# Requires the NBTAPI plugin. Nested keys can use dot notation.
key: item_value
# AUTO, BYTE, SHORT, INT, LONG, FLOAT, DOUBLE or STRING.
value-type: AUTO
# SET uses the NBT value as the price; MULTIPLY multiplies the current price by it.
# Invalid operations are reported and the modifier is ignored.
operation: SET
# Values outside this inclusive range, or longer than maximum-number-length, are ignored.
minimum-value: 0
maximum-value: 1000000
maximum-number-length: 64
mythic-changer:
type: match_item
enabled: false
# MAX, MIN or STACK. STACK applies matching rules in configuration order.
mode: STACK
rules:
named-item:
# ADD or MULTIPLY. Invalid operations are reported and this rule is ignored.
operation: MULTIPLY
# Supports math expressions and PlaceholderAPI. Invalid expressions are reported and ignored.
value: 1.2
match-item:
has-name: true
special-lore:
operation: ADD
# ADD adds this value to the price multiplier: 1 + 0.1 = 1.1.
value: 0.1
match-item:
contains-lore:
- 'Special'

give-item:
# Support value: BUKKIT, SMART
# SMART will cost more server performance but will follow the vanilla max stack to give player item, also support check full.
give-method: SMART
# Only support SMART give method.
check-full: true

📦Info

📊Data

Data Type

UltimateShop has 2 data types, they are: Player Data and Server Data.

Player data saves player self data, like each product buy times and sell times, cooldown times, and other things.

Server data saved server total product buy times, sell times and so on. Random placeholder's data will also saved in server data.

Save

For player data, we will auto save player data into database when player leave the server.

For server data, we will auto save server data when you are trying to stop the server normally. (Not directly close the console window, you need use /stop command in server)

Data not saved will be lost after server crash, so enable auto save feature if you don't want this.

Auto Save

You can use auto save feature so that plugin can store plugin data periodically to avoid data loss due to server crashes. It is not recommended to store at a high frequency, as this can cause server lag. You can find the following content in config.yml to set this feature:

Copy

auto-save:
enabled: true
hide-message: false
period-tick: 6000 # In ticks, 20 ticks = 1 second.

Database

You can find the following content in config.yml to set this feature:

Database

JDBC URL Example

JDBC Class

MySQL

jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/ultimateshop

com.mysql.cj.jdbc.Driver

MariaDB (Added in 4.1.4)

jdbc:mariadb://localhost:3306/ultimateshop

org.mariadb.jdbc.Driver

PostgreSQL (Added in 4.1.4)

jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/ultimateshop

org.postgresql.Driver

SQLLite (Added in 4.1.4)

jdbc:sqlite:plugins/UltimateShop/data.db

org.sqlite.JDBC

h2 (Added in 4.1.4)

jdbc:h2:file:./plugins/UltimateShop/data

org.h2.Driver

📦Info

❓FAQ

Q: Player don't cost money when buy product.

A: That is simply because you give self * permission so plugin will bypass price check for you. For common player, it will work correctly.

Q: My item can not be sold in shop, but I think they are same item.

A: No, actually they have small different, but you dind't find them. The simplest way is to change the sell-methods option in config.yml from Bukkit to ItemFormat.

Q: Does the /shop command has permission?

A: Use /shop can directly open a menu called main. This is a feature called Auto Open, and you can disable it at config.yml file with menu.auto-open.enabled option. If you only want some players to be exposed to this command, you can set conditions for the menu so that only players who meet the specified conditions can open the menu. For more info, please view Menus page.

Q: Why it say condition not meet when open daily example menu?

A: That menu already has a condition, which you can find in menus/shop_menu_templates/daily-shop-menu.yml.

Q: Why can I only sell 64x (or other amunt) items at once?

A: The person asking this question may never know a truth: before using the plugin, you should be familiar with the config.yml file. In config.yml, there is an option called sell.max-amount, and I want you to know its purpose by looking at its name.

Q: Selling reports that the 5-hour, daily, or weekly income limit is reached. How do I disable it?

A: It is not a toggle in config.yml. The limits are the three require-conditions shipped in the example shared_prices/vault-sell.yml template, 2,000 / 10,000 / 50,000 by default.

Delete the whole require-conditions section to remove all limits.

Delete one entry to remove only that period.

Raise value to loosen a limit.

To exempt a single product, add require-conditions: {} to its price entry.

The give-actions counters keep updating after the limits are removed, so the quota lines in the product lore keep growing. See Example: Sell Income Limits.

Q: How many custom placeholders can the free version create?

A: Up to 5; the premium version has no limit. On the free version, the sixth and later files in placeholders/custom_placeholders are not loaded and the console reports an error on startup. See Custom Placeholder.

Q: How to change the max amount limit of buy more menu?

A: There is a option called menu.buy-more.<buy more menu name>.max-amount option in config.yml file, or buy-more-menu.max-amount option in your product configs if you are setting up a separate buy more menu for a certain product.

Q: What does start-apply mean?

A: This means which times this price will apply. If you set it to 5, price will apply after player buy or sell this product 5 times.

This option will only work for ANY or ALL price-mode.

Q: Item dupe when quickly click item outside menu.

A: Some other plugin or mod lead to this problem. Try enable ignore-click-outside option jn config.yml file.

Q: What is different from free version and premium version?

A: Check this page for more info.

Q: Can I set different add lore for each product?

A: Yes, in Shop page we have told you that add-lore also works in each product configs!

Q: Shop menu can not open after use once!

A: Make sure your config.yml is latest format, if not, update it. Or, you are using menu cooldown system, disable it may fix.

Q: How can I translate item name in buy more menu and plugin message?

A: View Localized Item Name page.

Q: Why my item name is in Chinese?

A: Disable Localized Item Name feature.

Q: UltimateShop print Error:XXX message in console.

A: What I want to tell you is already put in the error message itself, like:

Error: Can not get prices section in your shop config!!

If there are no issues with the shop, there is no need to pay attention to it. The error message reported by the plugin is a prompt, and the plugin will automatically attempt to fix it once it detects this error.

Q: How to reset dynamic price?

A: View Dynamic Price page.

Previous🚀Performance

Next🆚Compare

📦Info

⚙️Install

Install

Put the .jar file into your server's plugins folder.

Stop your server and then restart it. Cannot load plugins in any other way while the server is starting.

When updating plugins, please be sure to remove old versions.

Previously, you used the free version, but now to upgrade to the paid version, you only need to install the paid version on the server and remove the free version. The configuration file of the plugin does not require any changes.

When downgrading from a new game version to an old version on the server where the plugin is located, it is important to remove the items folder from the configuration file.

When using the Localized Item Name feature, it is important to note that when switching server game versions, the previously generated localized files need to be deleted.

UltimateShop is just a shop plugin and does not provide custom economy functionality. If you need a custom economy as your server economy, please find a suitable economy plugin yourself. Vault is not an economy plugin, it is just a dependency plugin for many economy plugins. After installing Vault on the server, it is also necessary to install the economy plugins that support it.

Default configs are just helping you understand the config framework, you need modify them that meet your needs.

Previous✅Requirements

Next🔗Compatibility

📦Info

🚀Performance

UltimateShop is just a shop plugin that usually does not have too much impact on the server. It is usually a performance issue caused by your unreasonable settings or other plugins, such as:

Do not use ANY, ALL price/product mode unless necessary.

Different from CLASSIC_ANY, CLASSIC_ALL mode, ANY and ALL mode, the calculation for each buy or sell is independent. Only use the 2 mode when you set dynamic price or limit.

MMOItems/MythicMobs Item generation performance.

Both plugins are spend much server resource to generate item than vanilla items. If possible and your item is static item with no things like dynamic stat or level requirement, you can try use Save Item instead.

For suggestions below:
The change you need made are all exist in config.yml file.

Disable sell chest.

If you are not using sell chest feature, you can disable this feature to save serve performance.

Copy

sell:
sell-chest:
enabled: false

Set placeholder.click.enabled option to false.

This maybe save server performance.

Copy

click:
# If enabled, {buy-click} and {sell-stick} will display different value according to product status.
# But, it will maybe make server lag if you are running big server and have many products in your shop.
enabled: false

Set cooldown for menu system.

Recommend use when you are running big server, it will make player no longer quickly click and reopen shop menu to make sure UltimateShop not lag your server.

Do not enable auto update GUI feature.

Enabling these options will significantly increase server performance consumption, but buttons within the GUI will be able to automatically update.

In most cases, you just want the GUI to update and display the latest data when we reset buy times or sell times, so you can achieve this idea through use-times.auto-reset-mode option.

If you pursue ultimate plugin performance, it's best not to turn on this option either.

Use BUKKIT item give method.

This item give method has the best performance, but the cost is that there may be some issues with stacking items, and when the player's inventory is full, we can only throw the excess items on the ground instead of preventing the player from further trading.

Use Bukkit sell item match method.

This can save some server performance, but the drawback is that the items to be sold must be identical to the items in the shop, with no differences, even including anvil cost, enchants, etc. (Items with durability and enchantable items basically cannot be sold to the shop because of this)

Previous📊Data

Next❓FAQ

Last updated 4 months ago

Do not use ANY, ALL price/product mode unless necessary.

MMOItems/MythicMobs Item generation performance.

Disable sell chest.

Set placeholder.click.enabled option to false.

Set cooldown for menu system.

Do not enable auto update GUI feature.

Use BUKKIT item give method.

Use Bukkit sell item match method.

Copy

# Recommend use when you are running big server, it will make player no longer quickly click
# and reopen shop menu to make sure UltimateShop not lag your server.
# In ticks.
cooldown:
click: 6 # Default value is -1, set value greater than 5 is recommended.
reopen: 20 # Default value is -1, set value greater than 5 is recommended.

Copy

menu-update:
# Whether menu will refresh every buttons every 1 second.
# This will refresh placeholder that displayed in display item lore.
# But maybe lead to server lag if you have much online players, and they are all opening shop GUI.
circle-update: false
# Whether menu will refresh every buttons when click any of them.
# This will refresh placeholder that displayed in display item lore.
# But maybe lead to server lag if you have much online players, and they are all opening shop GUI.
click-update: false
# PREMIUM version only, if enabled, can update dynamic value used in GUI title.
title-update:
# Whether gui title will refresh every buttons every 1 second.
# This will refresh placeholder that displayed in menu title.
circle-update: false
# Whether gui title will refresh every buttons when click any of them.
# This will refresh placeholder that displayed in menu title.
click-update: true
resend-items-pack: false

Copy

auto-reset-mode: true

Copy

auto-reset-mode: false

Copy

give-item:
# Support value: BUKKIT, SMART
# SMART will cost more server performance but will follow the vanilla max stack to give player item, also support check full.
give-method: BUKKIT
# Only support SMART give method.
check-full: false

Copy

sell:
# Support Value: Bukkit or ItemFormat.
# For each product, you can add match-item section to make custom sell match method, for more info, please view Wiki.
sell-method: Bukkit

📦Info

✅Requirements

Java Version

Basic Requirement: Java16+

Java 17+ is recommended. Java17 and above versions are recommended, but plugins are compiled using Java16, so theoretically, you only need Java16 or higher versions.

Server Software

Server

Can work in your server

Can get offical support

Spigot

✅
Some features does not provide in Spigot servers

✅

Paper/Purpur

✅
Can provide subtle performance improvements.

✅

Folia

❓
Any problems only occurs in Folia servers may not be solved. Plugin is not designed for multi thread support.

❓

Folia's support is in the early testing stage and may be released in official versions or removed in the future. This support is not a guarantee.

Other softwares

❌

❌

Server Version

Version

Can work in your server

Can get offical support

Below 1.14

❌

❌

1.14 ~ 1.20.4

❓
The plugin can run on these versions, however, any issues caused on these versions will not be resolved.

❌

1.20.5 and above

✅
Using versions 1.21.5 and above is the most recommended, as you can enjoy the best features.

✅

If you encounter errors while using a certain version, please join our Discord feedback.

An economy plugin if you want to use custom currency

UltimateShop is just a shop plugin and does not provide custom economy functionality. If you need a custom economy as your server economy, please find a suitable economy plugin yourself. Vault is not an economy plugin, it is just a dependency plugin for many economy plugins. After installing Vault on the server, it is also necessary to install the economy plugins that support it.

🧭Menu Types

🔽Buy More Menus

Buy More menus let players choose a quantity for a selected product (quantities represent trade units—if one trade gives multiple items, the total scales accordingly). Players open them via a product's select-amount action or a buy_more_menu action with shop/product context.

Inventory quantity menus live in menus/buy_more_menus/ (built-in: buy-more.yml, buy-more-buy.yml, buy-more-sell.yml) and cannot be opened standalone via /shop menu.

Choose Menus and Caps

Global defaults in config.yml:

Copy

menu:
buy-more-menu:
not-open-when-invalid: true
display-item-max-stack: true
default:
menu: buy-more
max-amount: 693
only-buy:
menu: buy-more-buy
max-amount: 693
only-sell:
menu: buy-more-sell
max-amount: 693

menu.buy-more-menu Option

Purpose

default

Menu and max units when a product has both buy and sell prices

only-buy

Menu and max units when a product can only be bought

only-sell

Menu and max units when a product can only be sold

not-open-when-invalid

Blocks opening if the product has no valid confirm control

display-item-max-stack

Raises preview stack size up to 99 on Minecraft 1.20.5+

Shop settings.buy-more sets the default toggle, and individual products can override buy-more and buy-more-menu (selected quantity is clamped between 1 and max-amount, regardless of preview slot count):

The default click binding is menu.click-event.select-amount: SHIFT_RIGHT (set to NEVER to disable). The buy_more_menu action accepts shop and item, and Premium also allows per-open buy-more-menu.menu and buy-more-menu.max-amount overrides; see Custom Click Event.

Inventory Quantity Menu Example

Create a file in menus/buy_more_menus/ and reference its ID above:

Amount, preview, and confirm IDs must appear in layout, and each control type should use distinct IDs. Common buttons can still be used for filler or back buttons. The menu starts at quantity 1.

Quantity Menu Configuration Reference

Key

Behaviour

amount-items.<id>.display-item

Appearance of an amount adjustment button

amount-items.<id>.add-amount

Adds or subtracts from the current quantity

amount-items.<id>.set-amount

Sets a fixed quantity (used when add-amount is absent or 0)

display-items

Ordered preview symbols (takes priority over singular display-item)

display-item

Single preview symbol shorthand

display-items-padding-item

Filler for unused preview slots (Display Item format)

confirm-items.<id>.display-item

Confirm button appearance; supports {amount}

confirm-items.<id>.click-action

Fixed action (buy, sell, buy-or-sell, sell-all); omit to use the opening click's action

confirm-items.<id>.modify-lore

Generates price/info lore for the chosen quantity (default true)

Control menu-visibility

Per-presentation filtering

dialog.enabled

Allows native quantity input when Dialog is available

Preview items split across preview slots up to the active stack cap, with unused slots showing the padding item or air; confirming still runs normal trade validation (preview stacks are never given directly).

This file supports standard title, size, layout, buttons, and lifecycle actions, but does not inherit the source shop's settings.menu-settings or global graphical menu.ui. Its bedrock, arcmenu, ui, and display-items-ui keys do not define other quantity presentations.

Quantity Selection Across Presentations

Presentation

Quantity Controls

Text Source

Inventory

Amount buttons, preview, and confirm controls in this file

Current Buy More file

Bedrock Form

Text input followed by product info confirmation

Source shop menu bedrock.buy-or-sell → global menu.bedrock.buy-or-sell

Native Dialog

Native input if this file enables dialog.enabled, else Inventory

Source shop menu dialog.buy-more → global menu.dialog.buy-more

ArcMenu

Quantity controls inside the graphical product details panel

Graphical settings and ArcMenu text

In Bedrock Form, quantity input is part of the source shop flow and never renders Inventory slot controls. In Native Dialog, action-level menu and cap overrides persist across input and confirmation (global menu.force-ui can still override Dialog selection).

In ArcMenu, select-amount (default right-click) opens product details where +/- buttons, scroll wheel, or direct text quantity input adjust the count when Buy More and a quantity action are available. Inventory confirm-items labels do not rename graphical or native info actions.

Default Trade Amounts

Before a player picks a quantity, default-buy-amount and default-sell-amount apply independently; once a quantity is chosen, both buy and sell use that count. {buy-amount} and {sell-amount} reflect those trade counts, while explicit display-item stack sizes stay separate. See Default Trade Amounts.

Shop Menus · UI Modes · Display Item Add Lore

Quantity Selection Across Presentations

Default Trade Amounts

Copy

items:
A:
buy-more: true
buy-more-menu:
menu: buy-more
max-amount: 128

Copy

title: 'Choose amount'
size: 27
layout:
- '000000000'
- '01a2b3c00'
- '000D0E000'
dialog:
enabled: false
amount-items:
'1':
display-item:
material: RED_WOOL
name: '&c-1'
add-amount: -1
'2':
display-item:
material: GREEN_WOOL
name: '&a+1'
add-amount: 1
'3':
display-item:
material: PAPER
name: '&eSet to 64'
set-amount: 64
display-items: [a, b, c]
display-items-padding-item:
material: GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE
name: ' '
confirm-items:
D:
display-item:
material: EMERALD
name: '&aBuy x{amount}'
click-action: buy
modify-lore: true
E:
display-item:
material: GOLD_INGOT
name: '&eSell x{amount}'
click-action: sell
modify-lore: true

🧭Menu Types

❤️Favourite Menus - Premium

Favourite menus store each player's saved products and their order. Every list belongs to a specific favourite menu ID, so add-favourite actions must target that same ID. Saved products keep their source shop's prices and trade rules.

Files live in menus/favourite_menus/ (auto-detected by folder) and the built-in menu opens via /shop menu favourite or open_menu. Legacy files in common_menus can set menu-type: favourite (alias favorite) or use the global menu.favourite-gui.menu list.

Add Products to Favourites

The built-in add-favourite action binds to SWAP_OFFHAND (F) and targets the built-in favourite list:

Copy

type: add_favourite
menu: favourite
shop: '{shop}'
item: '{item}'

Use this in any action list with product context. For a custom list such as my-favourites, change menu to my-favourites (and ensure the product allows favouriting). Native and graphical info/extra-action controls automatically expose the configured action, though setting labels alone never creates a binding.

Inventory Example

Save as menus/favourite_menus/my-favourites.yml:

Copy

title: 'Favourites'
content: 'Your saved products.'
size: 27
layout:
- '000000000'
- '011111110'
- '0000D0000'
dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false
result-item: '1'
edit-mode-item:
D:
normal-display-item:
material: NAME_TAG
name: '&eEdit favourites'
editing-display-item:
material: WRITABLE_BOOK
name: '&aFinish editing'
empty-item:
display-item:
material: GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE
name: '&7Empty favourite slot'
result-lore:
- '&7Shop: {shop}'
- '&7Product: {product}'
editing-result-lore:
- '&eLeft: move forward'
- '&eRight: move backward'
- '&cDrop: remove'

Reload, add products to my-favourites, and run /shop menu my-favourites (global menu.force-ui overrides menu switches).

Favourite Configuration

Key

Purpose

result-item, result-items

Favourite product slot symbols (combined; defaults to '6' if omitted)

edit-mode-item.<id>.normal-display-item

Edit toggle appearance in normal mode

edit-mode-item.<id>.editing-display-item

Edit toggle appearance in edit mode

edit-mode-item.<id>.display-item

Fallback appearance when mode-specific items are omitted

edit-mode-item.<id>.slot

Zero-based fallback slot if the toggle ID is not in layout

empty-item.display-item

Placeholder item shown in unfilled result slots

result-lore

Normal-mode extra lore ({shop} and {product} show source)

editing-result-lore

Edit-mode hint lore

Special control menu-visibility

Per-presentation visibility

Special control ui

ArcMenu placement and appearance

Only one resolved edit toggle is used per menu. Root result-item slot count caps how many saved products are shown in saved order; graphical pagination does not increase capacity.

Edit-toggle text supports {editing}, {edit-mode}, {favourite-amount}, and {favorite-amount}. Empty-slot text supports those count aliases plus {slot-index} (1-based result position).

Normal vs. Edit Mode

Normal mode runs product click actions; clicking the edit toggle only switches modes without trading:

Inventory Input in Edit Mode

Action

Left-click

Move one slot forward

Right-click

Move one slot backward

Drop / Ctrl+Drop

Remove from this list

Removing a favourite never deletes player items or shop stock, and toggling edit mode off restores normal trading. The saved list and order are shared across all presentations.

Behaviour in Other Presentations

Presentation

Normal Mode

Edit Mode

Bedrock Form

Opens product info form

Selecting a product shows Forward / Backward / Remove / Back

Native Dialog

Product info and actions

Dedicated edit controls (empty result slots omitted)

ArcMenu

Product cards, actions, and details

Cards show Forward / Backward / Remove; supports Left / Right / Middle-click editing (Middle removes)

Bedrock Form can show configured empty-slot buttons. In ArcMenu, the edit toggle and navigation need ui.placement, while saved product cards use ui.products.placement (empty-slot graphical placement can be disabled).

Form edit text reads bedrock.favourite-edit (fallback menu.bedrock.favourite-edit); Dialog reads dialog.favourite-edit (menu.dialog.favourite-edit), including title, content, and buttons.forward/backward/remove/back (title/content support {item-name}). ArcMenu card edit labels default to the Form labels and can be overridden via ui.text.favourite-edit.forward/backward/remove; use presentation conditions for per-UI edit lore.

Favourite menus do not render Ore graphics (dialog.layout: ore falls back to native multi-action and generates no Ore resources).

UI Modes · Custom Click Event · Graphical UI Layout

🧭Menu Types

🔲Common Menus

Common menus display custom buttons for main hubs, category navigation, or info screens. Save them in menus/common_menus/ and open them with /shop menu <id> or the open_menu action.

Create a Navigation Menu

Save this as menus/common_menus/main.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Main Menu'
size: 9
layout:
- '000A0X000'
dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming
X:
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClose'
actions:
1:
type: close

Make sure the farming shop already exists (otherwise clicking the button will have no shop to open). Save the file, run /shop reload, and then run /shop menu main to open it.

Because dialog, bedrock, and arcmenu are all set to false in this example, it opens as a standard chest Inventory screen (unless global menu.force-ui is enabled in config.yml).

Opening a shop vs. opening another menu:

To open a shop (for buying/selling products), use type: shop_menu with shop: <shop-id>.

To open another Common, Search, Favourite, or Item Sell menu, use type: open_menu with menu: <menu-id>.

Common menus only show the buttons you define under buttons—they never pull in shop products automatically.

Common Menu Keys

Key

Purpose

title

Screen title

content

Screen description (fallback for presentation-specific content)

size, layout

Inventory size and slot layout

buttons

Button display items, actions, and conditions

conditions

Conditions checked before opening the menu

open-actions, close-actions

Actions run on menu open and close

dynamic-layout

Per-player layout resolution (Premium)

dialog, bedrock, arcmenu

Presentation switches and native options

ui

Ore / ArcMenu style, layout, and rendering settings

Keep the three condition scopes distinct: buttons.<id>.conditions checks clicks, buttons.<id>.display-conditions controls visibility, and root conditions controls opening the screen. See Layout & Buttons.

Use Other Presentations - Premium

To use native Dialog instead, replace the dialog block in the example with:

Also enable global menu.dialog.enabled in config.yml. For Ore, set dialog.layout: ore and configure graphical placement and the resource pack; for ArcMenu, install a compatible integration and enable global/menu arcmenu.enabled; for Bedrock Form, enable global/menu bedrock.enabled.

See UI Modes for selection order, forced UIs, and platform support, and Graphical UI Layout for areas and coordinates.

Custom Commands - Premium

Add custom-command at the menu root to register a command for that menu (avoid colliding with existing commands):

Inventory Refresh & Title Updates

In config.yml, menu.menu-update.circle-update refreshes item displays every second and click-update refreshes after clicks. Premium also supports dynamic title updates (menu.title-update.enabled / circle-update / click-update, requiring PacketEvents). See Inventory UI.

Declaring Special Menu Types in common_menus

Although Search, Favourite, and Item Sell menus should live in their dedicated folders (auto-detected by folder), legacy files in menus/common_menus/ can declare menu-type: search, favourite (alias favorite), or item-sell (alias item_sell) at the root (or be listed in the legacy global Search/Favourite lists).

Regardless of folder, available controls and UI support follow the resolved menu type.

Shop Menus · Menu Configuration & Inheritance

Inventory Refresh & Title Updates

Declaring Special Menu Types in common_menus

Copy

dialog:
enabled: true
layout: multi-action
content: '&7Choose a category'
button-width: 150
columns: 2

Copy

custom-command:
name: categories
description: Open the category menu

🧭Menu Types

💰Item Sell Menus

Item Sell menus accept physical items, preview the total payout, and wait for explicit confirmation. Closing without confirming returns all items; if you want items sold automatically on close, use the Sell All Menu.

Files live in menus/item_sell_menus/ (auto-detected by folder) and the built-in menu opens via /shop menu item-sell or open_menu. Legacy files in common_menus can declare menu-type: item-sell (alias item_sell) at the root.

Create an Item Deposit Menu

Save as menus/item_sell_menus/my-sell.yml, reload, and run /shop menu my-sell:

Copy

title: 'Sell Items'
size: 54
max-sell-amount: -1
layout:
- '000000000'
- '0IIIIIII0'
- '0IIIIIII0'
- '0IIIIIII0'
- '000000000'
- '0000C0X00'
input-item: 'I'
confirm-item: 'C'
confirm-button:
display-item:
material: EMERALD
name: '&aConfirm Sale'
lore:
- '&7Price: &f{price}'
- '&7Original price: &f{original-price}'
- '&7Multiplier: &fx{multiplier}'
- '&7Sellable items: &f{item-amount}'
- '&7Unsellable items: &f{unsellable-amount}'
buttons:
X:
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClose'
actions:
1:
type: close

I marks item deposit slots and C marks the confirm button; undefined 0 slots stay empty and do not accept items.

Configuration Reference

Key

Purpose

input-item

Primary input symbol (default 'I')

input-items

Additional input symbols (merged with input-item)

confirm-item

Confirm symbol (default 'C'; may occupy multiple slots)

confirm-button.display-item

Confirm button icon, name, and lore

max-sell-amount

Max combined trade units sold per confirmation (-1 for unlimited)

Supports standard title, layout, buttons, open conditions, and lifecycle actions; multi-character IDs follow Layout & Buttons. Note the confirm key is confirm-button, distinct from confirm-items in Buy More menus.

Price Preview and Confirmation

Confirm Button Placeholder

Meaning

{price}

Final payout after sell multipliers and item-based price modifiers

{original-price}

Payout before those multipliers/modifiers

{multiplier}

Combined effective multiplier

{item-amount}

Sellable item count

{unsellable-amount}

Unsellable item count

The preview updates live as items are added or removed. Confirming runs the full product sell flow (conditions, limits, events, actions, and transaction logs), and any unsold items remain in their slots and return on close (GUI icons are never treated as player items).

max-sell-amount caps only the combined units per confirmation in this menu—it never overrides product stock, conditions, or other sell limits.

Route Products That Inspect Actual Items Here

Products using price-modifier: true must inspect the physical items being sold, so configure their target Item Sell menu in config.yml:

sell and sell-all on those products route to the configured menu (the target ID must exist). In Form/Dialog, players still see product info first and choose Buy or Sell; choosing Sell opens the physical Inventory menu for deposit and confirmation.

Presentation Support

To support physical item deposits, Item Sell menus always use Inventory UI for all clients—never Form, Dialog, Ore, or ArcMenu—and do not inherit graphical menu.ui (even when menu.force-ui is enabled).

Price Modifier · Sell Multiplier · Inventory UI

price-modifier:
item-sell-menu:
enabled: true
menu: item-sell

🧭Menu Types

🔍Search Menus - Premium

Search menus let players find visible shop products by item and/or name. Inventory supports dropping a real item into an input slot or typing a chat keyword; Bedrock Form, Native Dialog, and ArcMenu each provide their own keyword input flow while keeping source shop prices and trade rules.

Files live in menus/search_menus/ (auto-detected by folder) and the built-in menu opens via /shop menu search or open_menu. Legacy files in common_menus can declare menu-type: search at the root or be listed in config.yml → menu.search-gui.menu.

Inventory Example

Save as menus/search_menus/my-search.yml:

Copy

title: 'Search'
content: 'Find a product.'
size: 27
layout:
- '000HNC000'
- '001111100'
- '066666660'
dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false
input-item: '1'
result-item: '6'
action-items:
H:
action-type: input-name
display-item:
material: HOPPER
name: '&eEnter a name'
C:
action-type: clear-search
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClear'
state-items:
N:
empty-input:
display-item:
material: PAPER
name: '&7Waiting for input'
has-input:
display-item:
material: COMPASS
name: '&a{showing-amount}/{result-amount} results'
no-result-item:
slot: 22
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cNo results'
result-lore:
- '&7Shop: {shop}'
- '&7Product: {product}'

Reload and run /shop menu my-search: place an item into 1 slots to filter by item, click H to enter a keyword in chat, and click C to clear (global menu.force-ui overrides menu switches).

Search Configuration

Key

Purpose

input-item, input-items

Physical item-filter symbols (combined; defaults to '1' if omitted)

result-item

Result slot symbol (defaults to '6'; count determines result capacity)

action-items.<id>.action-type

input-name (alias search-name) or clear-search

action-items.<id>.display-item

Special action button appearance

clear-search-item.<id>

Legacy clear action section (defaults to clear-search)

state-items.<id>.empty-input.display-item

State display before any input

state-items.<id>.has-input.display-item

State display when input is active

no-result-item.slot

Zero-based slot for the no-results item

no-result-item.display-item

No-results item appearance

result-lore

Extra lore on results ({shop} and {product} show source)

Common navigation belongs in buttons; place special action and state IDs in root layout, reserve input/result symbols, and set a valid no-result-item.slot (see Layout & Buttons).

State items support {result-amount} (total matches), {showing-amount} (currently shown), {input-amount} (deposited filter items), and {name-keyword}. These placeholders are for search state rendering only.

Keyword and Item Input

Item filter: placing an item in an input slot matches products associated with that item.

Name filter: entering a keyword matches product or item names (pure text search needs no deposited item); name matching uses Localized Item Name.

Global chat-prompt keywords in config.yml:

cancel keeps the previous keyword and clear resets it (built-in {lang} values follow the active language). Clearing search or closing/leaving the menu returns deposited filter items to the player.

Behaviour Across Presentations

Presentation

Input

Controls

Inventory

Physical item + chat keyword

Special actions, state, no-result item, and common buttons

Bedrock Form

Form text input for keyword

Search button and common navigation (no physical input/state slots)

Native Dialog

Native keyword input

Search submit and result info (no physical input slots)

ArcMenu

Chat keyword input

Graphical actions, state, no-result control, and product cards

In Form and Dialog, empty keywords show no products, reopening pre-fills the previous keyword, and cancelling preserves it; backing out of product info returns to search. Internal search submits/cancels do not re-run menu open-actions / close-actions—only leaving via normal navigation does.

Form labels read menu bedrock.search.* then global menu.bedrock.search.*; Dialog labels read dialog.search.* and menu.dialog.search.*.

Graphical Search

Search supports ArcMenu graphical UI: results use ui.products.placement, while special controls and common buttons use ui.placement. Root result-slot capacity still caps how many source results are collected—graphical pagination does not raise that cap.

Search does not render Ore graphics or generate Ore packs (dialog.layout: ore falls back to native multi-action). See UI Modes.

Favourite Menus · ArcMenu UI · Graphical UI Layout

Keyword and Item Input

Behaviour Across Presentations

Graphical Search

Copy

menu:
prompt:
cancel-keyword: cancel
search-gui:
prompt:
clear-keyword: clear

🧭Menu Types

🛒Sell All Menu

Sell All is a global Inventory screen where players deposit items and sell them automatically on close, with no confirm button. If you need a live price preview and explicit confirmation, use an Item Sell Menu.

Open the Menu

Players can run /shop sellall (requires ultimateshop.sellall), or trigger it from any menu button action:

Copy

actions:
1:
type: sell_all_menu

Even when triggered from Form, Dialog, Ore, or ArcMenu, it always opens a physical Inventory screen.

Configure the Screen

Configure it directly in config.yml under menu.sell-all (no file in menus/):

Copy

menu:
sell-all:
size: 54
title: '{lang}'
black-slots: []

menu.sell-all Key

Purpose

size

Slot count: 9, 18, 27, 36, 45, or 54

title

Screen title ({lang} uses the active language file)

black-slots

Zero-based slots blocked from item input (e.g. [0, 1, 2])

This screen has no menu ID and does not use layout, buttons, graphical styles, or force-ui.

Exclude Products

Exclude specific products in config.yml using shop-id;;product-id:

See Products for base pricing and sell rules.

Selling on Close

When the menu closes, the plugin scans eligible shop products and runs normal bulk selling on the deposited items (conditions, limits, ignore lists, and multipliers all apply). Global sell.max-amount caps the sell engine independently of Inventory capacity and Item Sell max-sell-amount.

Unsold or capped items return to the player's inventory (or drop if full); closing an empty menu gives no payout. Products requiring physical item inspection for price modifiers delegate to their configured Item Sell flow.

Item Sell Menus · Inventory UI

Previous💰Item Sell Menus

Next🎨UI Modes

Open the Menu

Configure the Screen

Exclude Products

Selling on Close

Copy

sell:
sell-all:
ignore-items:
- 'farming;;A'

🧭Menu Types

🛍️Shop Menus

Shop menus separate data from layout: shop files hold products, prices, and trade rules, while menu templates hold layout, navigation buttons, and presentation settings. Multiple shops can share one template.

Create a Shop and Template

Save the template as menus/shop_menu_templates/farming.yml:

Copy

title: '{shop-name}'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0X00'
dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false
buttons:
X:
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClose'
actions:
1:
type: close

Save the shop as shops/farming.yml:

With Vault and an economy plugin installed, run /shop reload and /shop menu farming. In Inventory UI, slot A shows shop product items.A and X shows the template's Close button (an enabled global menu.force-ui overrides presentation switches).

settings.menu: farming selects the template ID, while /shop menu farming opens the shop ID—they do not have to match. Shop templates are used by shops and cannot be opened standalone as Common menus.

Override a Template for One Shop

Use settings.menu-settings in the shop file to override title, presentation, or graphical style on top of a shared template:

Overrides merge recursively onto the template (lists such as layout are replaced whole), and global graphical defaults fill any missing ui fields. Shops that do not share a template can omit settings.menu and define their full screen inside settings.menu-settings. See Menu Configuration & Inheritance.

Add Products and Navigation

Define products under items in the shop and reference their IDs in the template layout. Navigation buttons can live in template buttons (shared) or shop buttons (shop-specific); avoid duplicate IDs across sources.

Product clicks use menu.click-event and per-product overrides, while common buttons run their actions. Graphical layout changes appearance and coordinates only—it never alters Products pricing or trade logic.

Fill Product Slots Across Shops with shop-items

In a shop menu template, shop-items marks which layout characters act as generic product slots. For example, the four P slots below fill left-to-right with the first four products in the shop's items section (unused slots stay empty):

To bind fixed positions to the 1st, 2nd, and 3rd products, use shop-items: ['A', 'B', 'C'].

Other slots can still hold common buttons (like X) or explicit product IDs, and product display conditions and visibility still apply. Individual shops can override shop-items via settings.menu-settings.shop-items (called Shop Item Symbols in the in-game editor).

Add Page Buttons to an Inventory Shop

When using a repeated shop-items symbol, add previous-page-item.<char> and next-page-item.<char> keyed by their layout characters. Each page holds as many products as there are matching product slots in layout:

By default, Previous hides on page 1 and Next hides on the last page (showing hidden-item if configured, or air). Set hide-on-first-page / hide-on-last-page to false to keep the arrows visible (clicking at the boundary stays on the same page). Pagination swaps product slots only and leaves common buttons in place; both buttons can also be edited in the in-game editor.

Present the Same Shop Across UIs - Premium

The same shop can be shown in multiple client UIs, each reading its own layout settings:

Inventory: uses the root size and layout slots.

Bedrock Form & Native Dialog: turns the active products and buttons into a list or action controls.

Ore & ArcMenu (Graphical Shops): uses the chosen style's ui.layout, places product cards in ui.products.placement, and places common buttons in their own ui.placement.

In Ore / ArcMenu graphical shops, which products appear depends on whether dynamic-layout is enabled:

With dynamic-layout off (default): any eligible product in the shop's items section appears in the graphical product area automatically—even if you did not put its ID in the chest layout.

With dynamic-layout: true: only products picked by the layout rules appear (unless another product explicitly sets its own ui.placement).

Common buttons (Back, Close, navigation, etc.): never appear automatically in graphical UIs—you must always give them their own ui.placement. Every entry still respects menu-visibility and display-conditions.

UI Modes · Graphical UI Layout · Buy More Menus

Next🔽Buy More Menus

settings:

menu: farming
items:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&eWheat'
products:
1:
material: WHEAT
buy-prices:
1:
economy-plugin: Vault
amount: 5

Copy

menu: farming
menu-settings:
title: '&aFarming Specials'
dialog:
enabled: true
layout: multi-action
bedrock:
enabled: true
content: '&7Browse farming products'
ui:
style: bedrock

Copy

size: 9
shop-items: ['P']
layout:
- '0PPPP000X'

Copy

size: 18
shop-items: ['P']
layout:
- '0PPPPPPP0'
- 'l0000000r'

previous-page-item:
l:

display-item:
material: ARROW
name: '&ePrevious Page'
hidden-item:
material: GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE
name: ' '

r:
hide-on-last-page: true
display-item:
material: ARROW
name: '&eNext Page'

🖥️Menu UI Modes

✨ArcMenu UI - Premium

ArcMenu renders Common, Shop, Search, and Favourite menus as interactive graphical screens for Java players. It shares styles, named areas, placement, and product card settings with Ore, while using its own ArcMenu integration and client setup.

Enable ArcMenu

Install a compatible ArcMenu plugin and complete its client resource setup (UltimateShop targets the ArcMenu 3.4.3 API, including native text input).

Enable global settings in config.yml:

Copy

menu:
arcmenu:
enabled: true
ui:
style: classic

Save as menus/common_menus/categories.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Categories'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0000'
arcmenu:
enabled: true
content: '&7Choose a category'
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming
ui:
appearance: button
placement:
area: sidebar
order: 0

Make sure the farming shop already exists. Save your config, run /shop reload, and run /shop menu categories (the button appears in the chosen style's sidebar area). Under normal selection, Java tries ArcMenu first, then Dialog/Ore, then Inventory; see UI Modes.

Styles & Presets

Select classic or bedrock via global menu.ui.style or menu ui.style, or copy a file in plugins/UltimateShop/ui/ to create your own:

Preset

Visual Style & Layout

classic

Left vertical sidebar + right product grid

bedrock

Charcoal panels, beveled controls, green buy/hover states, light sell controls, top 5-button navigation bar + 3-column product grid

Note that bedrock also names its top horizontal bar sidebar (sidebar is just an area ID, not a fixed screen edge). When switching presets, update any hardcoded row/column indices or custom area coordinates.

Menu fields override style defaults, while menu.force-ui.style can override local defaults; see Menu Configuration & Inheritance and Graphical UI Layout.

Why a Button Appears in Inventory but Not ArcMenu

ArcMenu skips common buttons that lack ui.placement. Root layout only positions Inventory slots; products inherit ui.products.placement, whereas common buttons must set their own graphical placement.

For example, to show legacy shop-link arrows in ArcMenu, add:

If the button is defined under a shop's buttons, keep its symbol in the shop menu layout as well. For multi-page products inside one shop, use ui.layout.pagination instead of manual page arrows (previous-page-item / next-page-item are Inventory-only).

Product Actions & Quantity Controls

Product cards show up to two available actions from ui.products.actions.primary and remaining/listed actions in ui.products.actions.extra (filtered by existing click bindings, prices, conditions, and buy-only/sell-only rules).

Right-clicking a product defaults to select-amount (customizable via ui.renderers.arcmenu.right-click-action), opening the graphical product details panel rather than an Inventory Buy More file. When Buy More and a quantity action are available, +/- buttons and the scroll wheel adjust the count (Inventory amount-items and preview slots do not style graphical details).

A stack count > 1 on the item icon reflects the display item's stack size, independent of the selected trade units.

Text Quantity Input

When ArcMenu's text input service is available, clicking the number between - and + lets players type a quantity directly (even when ArcMenu hides the vanilla anvil UI, live input appears inside the focused graphical field).

Finishing input (Esc in mouse mode) clamps valid integers between 1 and the Buy More cap; invalid input keeps the previous count. Players still click Buy or Sell to execute the trade. Prompt text comes from ui.text.amount-input (requires Buy More enabled, a valid quantity menu, and select-amount).

ArcMenu Renderer Settings

Per-menu settings live under ui.renderers.arcmenu:

Relative Key

Purpose

right-click-action

Action triggered on product right-click

screen-scale

Overall graphical scale

animation.enabled / duration

Open animation (skipped when reopening the same menu)

more-menu.columns

Column count for the extra-actions popup

tooltip.enabled / width / font-size / padding

Hover tooltip appearance

The refresh interval always reads from global config.yml in ticks (minimum 5, or from the global style; per-menu update-interval is ignored):

Backgrounds, Decorations, and Tooltips

ArcMenu reads colors from resolved ui.theme (window and title backgrounds toggle independently, and theme.decorations controls frames, corner accents, and header dividers; see Theme and Text).

Tooltips lay out formatted lore as left-aligned blocks and scale down if oversized; mouse mode adapts to surface bounds, cursor sensitivity, player preference, margin, and vertical pitch limits, while touch/Vision mode uses surface bounds.

Menu-Type Behaviour

Search & Favourite: use ArcMenu graphical layouts and dedicated controls (source capacity is still capped by root result slots); Favourite edit mode provides reorder and remove controls.

Buy More: handled inside the product details panel.

Item Sell / Sell All: remain physical Inventory screens.

ArcMenu never uses /shop dialogpack (that command builds Ore assets only).

Search Menus · Favourite Menus · Buy More Menus

🖥️Menu UI Modes

🛏️Bedrock Form UI - Premium

Bedrock Form UI converts menu entries into native Bedrock list buttons and provides dedicated product info and quantity-input forms. It shares products, prices, and trade rules with Java screens without using graphical ui.layout coordinates.

Enable Forms

Requires a supported Geyser / Floodgate setup that detects Bedrock players. Configure config.yml:

Copy

menu:
bedrock:
enabled: true
check-method: FLOODGATE
not-auto-close: true
auto-add-icon:
enabled: true

Enable on a Common menu (e.g. menus/common_menus/categories.yml):

Copy

title: '&8Categories'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0000'
bedrock:
enabled: true
content: '&7Choose a category'
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming

Make sure the farming shop already exists. After reloading, join with a Bedrock client and run /shop menu categories to see the form. Under normal selection, both global and menu bedrock.enabled must be true (global menu.force-ui overrides switches while keeping platform and integration requirements).

Entry List and Visibility

Forms collect entries from root layout and ignore chest spacer gaps; shop products appear before common navigation buttons. Clicking a common button runs LEFT click, while clicking a product opens its info form by default.

Hide filler panes with menu-visibility.form: false; entries whose active display item has bedrock.hide: true or an empty button name are also omitted.

Icons and Extra Text

Configure Bedrock Form icons and extra lines inside the active display-item:

display-item.bedrock Key

Meaning

icon

path;;<client texture path> or url;;<public image URL>

extra-line

Extra line below the button title (products support price placeholders)

hide

Hides this display variant in Forms

When icon is omitted, bedrock.auto-add-icon attempts to use vanilla textures (Java custom-model-data does not provide Bedrock textures automatically). For conditional display items, place bedrock options inside each variant.

For products, explicit display-item.bedrock.extra-line wins ('' intentionally leaves it blank); otherwise the plugin reads menu bedrock.price-extra-line then global menu.bedrock.price-extra-line:

This is independent of graphical ui.products.price.

Direct Product Actions

To execute a trade directly instead of opening the info form, add bedrock.click-event at the product root in the shop file:

Lookup order: product bedrock.click-event → owning shop menu bedrock.click-event → global menu.bedrock.click-event (applies to LEFT clicks only; trade conditions and item-inspection sell routing still apply).

Form Text & Option Reference

Options read menu bedrock.* first, then global menu.bedrock.* (check-method is global-only):

Relative Key

Purpose

enabled

Allows Bedrock Form

content

Screen description (falls back to root content)

check-method

Global detection method: FLOODGATE or UUID

not-auto-close

Reopens supported info forms after a trade

auto-add-icon.enabled / format

Vanilla icon generation (supports {version}, {path})

price-extra-line.default / only-buy / only-sell

Product price extra lines

click-event

Default direct product action

info.title, info.buttons.buy/sell/buy-more/sell-all/back

Product info form text

buy-or-sell.title, buy-or-sell.buttons.amount.name/tip

Quantity input form text

search.input, search.buttons.search

Search form input and button

favourite-edit.title/content/buttons.*

Favourite edit form text

{lang} resolves from the active language file. Form product actions come from available click actions—graphical ui.products.actions and Inventory Buy More confirm labels do not define native Form controls.

Search and Favourite menus use dedicated Form flows, Item Sell and Sell All keep physical Inventory, and graphical ui.style: bedrock is an Ore/ArcMenu visual preset unrelated to Bedrock Forms.

UI Modes · Buy More Menus · Search Menus · Favourite Menus

default: '&7Buy: {buy-price} | Sell: {sell-price}'
only-buy: '&7Buy: {buy-price}'
only-sell: '&7Sell: {sell-price}'

🖥️Menu UI Modes

💬Native Dialog UI - Premium

Native Dialog uses the Paper 1.21.9+ Dialog API to present native action buttons or item-action lists to Java players. Native dialog layouts do not require an Ore resource pack (Ore is a separate graphical layout built on top of the dialog transport).

Enable Native Dialog

Configure global defaults in config.yml:

Copy

menu:
dialog:
enabled: true
not-auto-close: true
auto-add-sprite:
enabled: true
auto-hide: true
format: '<sprite:"{namespace}:{atlas}":{path}>'

Save a Common menu as menus/common_menus/categories.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Categories'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0000'
dialog:
enabled: true
layout: multi-action
content: '&7Choose a category'
button-width: 150
columns: 2
arcmenu:
enabled: false
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming

Make sure the farming shop already exists. Run /shop reload, then run /shop menu categories on a supported Java 1.21.9+ client. Under normal selection, both global and menu dialog.enabled must be true (note: ArcMenu takes priority if also enabled and available; global menu.force-ui overrides switches).

Native Layouts

Menu dialog.layout

Result

multi-action

Native multi-action button grid

item-action-list

Native item-action list

ore

Ore graphical UI for supported menu types

Root layout supplies entry order while ignoring empty spacer slots; individual entries can override native item-action behaviour via their own dialog.layout. Visibility uses menu-visibility.dialog or menu-visibility.item-action-list depending on the active layout.

To hide a button's sprite icon, add this at the button root (not inside display-item or ui):

Global auto-add-sprite.auto-hide: true prevents duplicate sprites when an entry already displays an item icon.

Dialog Text & Controls

Menu dialog Key

Purpose

enabled

Allows Dialog

layout

Selects native layout or ore

content

Dialog body (falls back to root content)

button-width, columns

Native action button width and columns (defaults 150, 2)

not-auto-close

Keeps supported info dialogs open after an action

default-button

Fallback exit label

info.display-item, info.title

Product info appearance

info.buttons.buy/sell/buy-more/sell-all/back

Info action labels

buy-more.display-item/title/input/buttons.confirm

Native quantity input flow

search.input, search.buttons.search

Search controls

favourite-edit.title/content/buttons.*

Favourite edit controls

details

Ore product details label

Shared text and behaviour options fall back to global menu.dialog.* (content, button-width, and columns are read from the menu itself; auto-add-sprite is global-only). All labels support language references and placeholders.

Product & Quantity Flow

Selecting a product shows its available actions filtered by click bindings, prices, and eligibility (ui.products.actions configures graphical cards, not native dialog buttons).

When the selected Buy More menu enables dialog.enabled and the product supports select-amount, native quantity input opens (labels come from source shop dialog.buy-more); otherwise it falls back to Inventory Buy More. The chosen quantity and any per-open menu/cap overrides persist through input and confirmation.

Search & Favourites

Search provides keyword input and result info, while Favourites provide browse and edit controls—neither converts physical input slots into native controls, and dialog.layout: ore resolves to native multi-action.

Ore Layout

Set dialog.layout: ore on a Common or Shop menu and configure graphical areas/placement under ui (requires generating the resource pack; native button-width and columns do not position Ore cards).

UI Modes · Buy More Menus · Search Menus · Favourite Menus

🖥️Menu UI Modes

📦Inventory UI

Inventory UI displays standard chest-style slots on both Java and Bedrock, reading root size, layout, and buttons (graphical ui.layout and ui.placement do not affect Inventory slots).

Select Inventory UI

For a single menu, disable other presentations at the root:

Copy

dialog:
enabled: false
bedrock:
enabled: false
arcmenu:
enabled: false

For all menus, force Inventory globally in config.yml:

Copy

menu:
force-ui:
enabled: true
type: inventory
style: ''

Global force-ui overrides per-menu switches and restores normal selection when disabled. Inventory is also the standard fallback whenever a requested presentation is unavailable.

Configure Rows and Entries

Save as menus/common_menus/example.yml, reload, and run /shop menu example:

Each row has 9 slots (size must be 9, 18, 27, 36, 45, or 54). Undefined IDs stay empty, '0' can be defined as a filler button, and multi-character IDs use backticks; see Layout & Buttons.

Clicks, Visibility, and Refresh

Common buttons run actions after click conditions pass, while shop products use configured product click actions. Use menu-visibility.inventory: false to hide decorative entries only in Inventory, or use Premium dynamic-layout and display-conditions for per-player slots.

Global refresh and title-update options in config.yml:

circle-update refreshes every second; click-update refreshes after clicks (enable periodic refresh only when placeholders change over time).

title-update is a Premium feature requiring PacketEvents.

Inventory click and reopen cooldowns use menu.cooldown.click and menu.cooldown.reopen in ticks (-1 disables).

Screens That Always Use Inventory

Item Sell accepts physical items and waits for confirmation, while Sell All sells deposited items on close—both always use Inventory regardless of global UI settings. Buy More uses its own quantity controls and item previews.

UI Modes · Display Item Add Lore

Clicks, Visibility, and Refresh

Screens That Always Use Inventory

Copy

title: '&8Example'
size: 18
layout:
- '000000000'
- '0000X0000'
buttons:
X:
display-item:
material: BARRIER
name: '&cClose'
actions:
1:
type: close

Copy

menu:
menu-update:
circle-update: false
click-update: false
title-update:
enabled: false
circle-update: false
click-update: true
resend-items-pack: false

🖥️Menu UI Modes

🪨Ore UI - Premium

Ore renders graphical product cards and navigation controls for Java players using Paper's Dialog transport and a generated client resource pack. Supported for Common and Shop menus on Paper 1.21.9+, sharing graphical layouts and styles with ArcMenu.

Enable Ore

Configure global dialog and Ore settings in config.yml:

Copy

menu:
dialog:
enabled: true
ore:
show-item-icon:
enabled: true
auto-hide-one: true
pack-format: 0
ui:
style: classic

Save as menus/common_menus/categories.yml:

Copy

title: '&8Categories'
size: 9
layout:
- '0000A0000'
dialog:
enabled: true
layout: ore
content: '&7Choose a category'
arcmenu:
enabled: false
buttons:
A:
display-item:
material: WHEAT
name: '&aFarming'
actions:
1:
type: shop_menu
shop: farming
ui:
appearance: button
placement:
area: sidebar
order: 0

Make sure the farming shop already exists. Run /shop reload, generate and load the resource pack below, and then run /shop menu categories (the button uses the chosen style's sidebar area; global menu.force-ui overrides switches).

Generate the Resource Pack

Run /shop reload after editing YAML so the plugin loads the latest menu definitions.

Run /shop dialogpack (requires ultimateshop.dialogpack) to build assets for Ore-enabled menus.

Merge the generated files in plugins/UltimateShop/pack/ into the resource pack distributed to players.

Load the updated resource pack on the client before reopening modified screens.

Use /shop dialogpack <format> to specify a pack format (69–10000); global menu.dialog.ore.pack-format: 0 uses the built-in format for your server version.

Whenever you change graphical layouts, themes, templates, or icons, you must rerun /shop dialogpack and redistribute the pack—reloading YAML alone does not push new assets to clients.

Pack generation covers Ore-enabled Common and Shop menus (including forced Ore); Search and Favourite fall back to native Dialog and generate no Ore assets.

Layout & Appearance Keys

Key

Purpose

dialog.enabled, dialog.layout: ore

Selects Ore under normal selection

dialog.content

Header content (falls back to root content)

ui.style

Shared graphical preset

ui.layout.canvas/header/areas/pagination

Canvas, headers, named areas, and pagination

ui.products.placement

Default product card area and placement

ui.products.card

Card icon size, padding, and Ore body line limits

ui.products.price / actions

Compact price line and card action buttons

Entry ui.placement, ui.appearance

Per-button/product placement and button / card look

ui.theme

Shared colors

ui.renderers.ore.templates

Resource pack card texture templates

Global menu.dialog.ore.show-item-icon.*

Item icon and stack-count display

Global menu.dialog.ore.pack-format

Pack metadata pack_format

Root Inventory layout only identifies entries—it does not set graphical coordinates. Common buttons need explicit ui.placement, while products default to ui.products.placement. See Graphical UI Layout.

Custom Template Textures

Place PNG source files under plugins/UltimateShop/textures/ (for example textures/shop/card.png) and reference them:

Paths are relative to textures/ (no absolute paths or ..), and hex colors must be quoted. Rebuild and redistribute the resource pack after editing textures.

Text Rendering & Menu-Type Support

Ore uses native text rendering, so ArcMenu font scaling will not produce identical results in Ore. ui.products.card.lore-lines and description-lines cap visible lines on the card face while keeping full text in hover tooltips.

When Ore is selected: Search and Favourite use native multi-action; Buy More uses native quantity input or Inventory; Item Sell, Sell All, and the In-Game Editor stay physical Inventory.

Native Dialog · ArcMenu · UI Modes

Next✨ArcMenu UI - Premium

ui:
renderers:
ore:
templates:
product:
texture: 'shop/card.png'
selected-texture: 'shop/card-selected.png'
selected-background: '#244A32'
selected-border: '#65D98B'
